# --------------------------------------------------

def update_conversation_title(
    conn,
    conversation_id: str,
    user_email: str,
    encrypted_title: str,
):
    cursor = conn.cursor()

    try:
        cursor.execute(
            """
            UPDATE chat_conversations
            SET
                encrypted_title = %s,
                updated_at = %s
            WHERE id = %s
              AND user_email = %s
            """,
            (
                encrypted_title,
                datetime.utcnow(),
                conversation_id,
                user_email,
            ),
        )

        conn.commit()

        return cursor.rowcount > 0

    finally:
        cursor.close()


# --------------------------------------------------
# Add Message
# --------------------------------------------------

def add_message(
    conn,
    conversation_id: str,
    role: str,
    encrypted_content: str,
    message_type: str = "chat",
):
    message_id = str(uuid.uuid4())

    cursor = conn.cursor()

    try:
        cursor.execute(
            """
            INSERT INTO chat_messages
            (
                id,
                conversation_id,
                role,
                encrypted_content,
                message_type,
                created_at
            )
            VALUES
            (
                %s,
                %s,
                %s,
                %s,
                %s,
                %s
            )
            """,
            (
                message_id,
                conversation_id,
                role,
                encrypted_content,
                message_type,
                datetime.utcnow(),
            ),
        )

        cursor.execute(
            """
            UPDATE chat_conversations
            SET updated_at = %s
            WHERE id = %s
            """,
            (
                datetime.utcnow(),
                conversation_id,
            ),
        )

        conn.commit()

        return message_id

    finally:
        cursor.close()


# --------------------------------------------------
# Update Message
# --------------------------------------------------

def update_message(
    conn,
    message_id: str,
    conversation_id: str,
    encrypted_content: str,
):
    cursor = conn.cursor()

    try:
        cursor.execute(
            """
            UPDATE chat_messages
            SET
                encrypted_content = %s
            WHERE id = %s
              AND conversation_id = %s
            """,
            (
                encrypted_content,
                message_id,
                conversation_id,
            ),
        )

        conn.commit()

        return cursor.rowcount > 0

    finally:
        cursor.close()


# --------------------------------------------------
# Get Conversation Messages
# --------------------------------------------------

def get_messages(
    conn,
    conversation_id: str,
):
    cursor = conn.cursor(
        cursor_factory=RealDictCursor,
    )

    try:
        cursor.execute(
            """
            SELECT
                id,
                conversation_id,
                role,
                encrypted_content,
                message_type,
                created_at
            FROM chat_messages
            WHERE conversation_id = %s
            ORDER BY created_at ASC
            """,
            (
                conversation_id,
            ),
        )

        return cursor.fetchall()

    finally:
        cursor.close()

# --------------------------------------------------

def delete_conversation(
    conn,
    conversation_id: str,
    user_email: str,
):
    cursor = conn.cursor()

    try:
        cursor.execute(
            """
            DELETE FROM chat_conversations
            WHERE id = %s
              AND user_email = %s
            """,
            (
                conversation_id,
                user_email,
            ),
        )

        conn.commit()

        return cursor.rowcount > 0

    finally:
        cursor.close()



########################################################################################################################
FILE: app/conversation/router.py
FULL_PATH: /opt/AIDA/aida_react/backend/app/conversation/router.py
########################################################################################################################

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException

from app.auth.dependencies import get_authorization_context
from app.auth.schema import AuthorizationContext
from app.session.session_service import SessionService
from app.conversation.schema import ContextUpdateRequest
from app.exchange_trigger.trigger_service import TriggerService
from app.conversation.service import ConversationService


router = APIRouter(
    prefix="/api/conversations",
    tags=["Conversations"],
)

session_service = SessionService()
trigger_service = TriggerService()
conversation_service = ConversationService()


@router.post("")
async def create_conversation(
    authorization: AuthorizationContext = Depends(
        get_authorization_context
    ),
):
    session_id = session_service.create_session(
        authorization.session_id
    )

    return {
        "session_id": session_id
    }


@router.get("/{session_id}")
async def get_conversation(
    session_id: str,
    authorization: AuthorizationContext = Depends(
        get_authorization_context
    ),
):
    conversation = session_service.get_owned_session(
        session_id=session_id,
        user_session_id=authorization.session_id,
    )

    if conversation is None:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=404,
            detail="Conversation not found."
        )

    return {
        "session_id": session_id,
        "context": {
            "oem": conversation.get("oem"),
            "project_key": conversation.get("project_key"),
            "process_name": conversation.get("process_name"),
        },
        "last_query": conversation.get("last_query"),
        "last_response": conversation.get("last_response"),
    }



@router.put("/{session_id}/context")
async def update_conversation_context(
    session_id: str,
    request: ContextUpdateRequest,
    authorization: AuthorizationContext = Depends(
        get_authorization_context
    ),
):
    # 1. Verify that this conversation belongs to
    #    the currently authenticated user.
    conversation = session_service.get_owned_session(
        session_id=session_id,
        user_session_id=authorization.session_id,
    )

    if conversation is None:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=404,
            detail="Conversation not found.",
        )

    # 2. Validate the project if supplied.
    if request.project_key is not None:
        authorized_project_keys = {
            project.project_key
            for project in authorization.projects
        }

        if request.project_key not in authorized_project_keys:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=403,
                detail="Project is not authorized.",
            )
        
    # 3. Validate the process if supplied.
    if request.process_name is not None:
        if request.project_key is None:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=400,
                detail="A project must be selected before selecting a process."
            )

        selected_project = next(
            (
                project
                for project in authorization.projects
                if project.project_key == request.project_key
            ),
            None,
        )

        if selected_project is None:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=403,
                detail="Project is not authorized."
            )

        process_list = trigger_service.get_process_list(
            [selected_project.db_project_id]
        )

        authorized_processes = {
            process.get("process_name")
            for process in process_list.get("processes", [])
        }

        if request.process_name not in authorized_processes:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=403,
                detail="Process is not authorized for the selected project."
            )
            

    # 4. Update the conversation context.
    updated_context = session_service.update_context(
        session_id=session_id,
        new_oem=request.oem,
        new_project=request.project_key,
        new_process=request.process_name,
    )

    if updated_context is None:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=404,
            detail="Conversation not found.",
        )

    return {
        "status": "success",
        "context": {
            "oem": updated_context.get("oem"),
            "project_key": updated_context.get("project_key"),
            "process_name": updated_context.get("process_name"),
        },
    }

from typing import Optional

from pydantic import BaseModel


class ContextUpdateRequest(BaseModel):
    oem: Optional[str] = None
    project_key: Optional[str] = None
    process_name: Optional[str] = None


class RenameConversationRequest(BaseModel):
    title: str


class UpdateMessageRequest(BaseModel):
    content: str



########################################################################################################################
FILE: app/conversation/service.py
FULL_PATH: /opt/AIDA/aida_react/backend/app/conversation/service.py
########################################################################################################################

from app.db.connection import get_pg_connection
from app.conversation import repository
from app.security.encryption import encryption_service

import json


class ConversationService:

    # --------------------------------------------------
    # Create Conversation
    # --------------------------------------------------

    def create_conversation(
        self,
        user_email: str,
        title: str | None = None,
    ):

        encrypted_title = None

        if title:
            encrypted_title = encryption_service.encrypt(title)

        with get_pg_connection() as conn:

            conversation_id = repository.create_conversation(
                conn=conn,
                user_email=user_email,
                encrypted_title=encrypted_title,
            )

        return conversation_id


    # --------------------------------------------------
    # Get Owned Conversation
    # --------------------------------------------------

    def get_owned_conversation(
        self,
        conversation_id: str,
        user_email: str,
    ):

        with get_pg_connection() as conn:

            conversation = repository.get_owned_conversation(
                conn=conn,
                conversation_id=conversation_id,
                user_email=user_email,
            )

        if not conversation:
            return None

        return self._decrypt_conversation(conversation)


    # --------------------------------------------------
    # Get User Conversations
    # --------------------------------------------------

    def get_user_conversations(
        self,
        user_email: str,
        limit: int = 20,
    ):

        with get_pg_connection() as conn:

            conversations = repository.get_user_conversations(
                conn=conn,
                user_email=user_email,
                limit=limit,
            )

        return [
            self._decrypt_conversation(conversation)
            for conversation in conversations
        ]


    # --------------------------------------------------
    # Update Conversation Context
    # --------------------------------------------------

    def update_conversation_context(
            self,
            conversation_id: str,
            user_email: str,
            oem: str | None,
            project_key: str | None,
            process_name: str | None,
        ) -> bool:

        with get_pg_connection() as conn:
            conversation = repository.get_owned_conversation(
                conn=conn,
                conversation_id=conversation_id,
                user_email=user_email,
            )

            if not conversation:
                return False

            context_changed = (
                conversation.get("oem") != oem
                or conversation.get("project_key") != project_key
                or conversation.get("process_name") != process_name
            )

            updated = repository.update_conversation_context(
                conn=conn,
                conversation_id=conversation_id,
                user_email=user_email,
                oem=oem,
                project_key=project_key,
                process_name=process_name,
            )

            if not updated:
                return False

            if context_changed:
                context_content = json.dumps(
                    {
                        "old": {
                            "oem": conversation.get("oem"),
                            "project_key": conversation.get("project_key"),
                            "process_name": conversation.get("process_name"),
                        },
                        "new": {
                            "oem": oem,
                            "project_key": project_key,
                            "process_name": process_name,
                        },
                    }
                )

                repository.add_message(
                    conn=conn,
                    conversation_id=conversation_id,
                    role="assistant",
                    encrypted_content=encryption_service.encrypt(
                        context_content
                    ),
                    message_type="context_change",
                )
            return {
                "changed": context_changed,
                "old": {
                    "oem": conversation.get("oem"),
                    "project_key": conversation.get("project_key"),
                    "process_name": conversation.get("process_name"),
                },
                "new": {
                    "oem": oem,
                    "project_key": project_key,
                    "process_name": process_name,
                },
            }


    # --------------------------------------------------
    # Update Conversation Title
    # --------------------------------------------------

    def update_conversation_title(
        self,
        conversation_id: str,
        user_email: str,
        title: str,
    ):

        encrypted_title = encryption_service.encrypt(title)

        with get_pg_connection() as conn:

            updated = repository.update_conversation_title(
                conn=conn,
                conversation_id=conversation_id,
                user_email=user_email,
                encrypted_title=encrypted_title,
            )

        return updated


    # --------------------------------------------------
    # Add Message
    # --------------------------------------------------

    def add_message(
        self,
        conversation_id: str,
        user_email: str,
        role: str,
        content: str,
        message_type: str = "chat",
    ):

        if role not in {"user", "assistant"}:
            raise ValueError(
                "Invalid message role."
            )

        # Verify ownership before storing the message.
        conversation = self.get_owned_conversation(
            conversation_id=conversation_id,
            user_email=user_email,
        )

        if not conversation:
            return None

        encrypted_content = encryption_service.encrypt(
            content
        )

        with get_pg_connection() as conn:

            message_id = repository.add_message(
                conn=conn,
                conversation_id=conversation_id,
                role=role,
                encrypted_content=encrypted_content,
                message_type=message_type,
            )


            # Auto-generate title
            if role == "user" and not conversation.get("title"):
                title = self.generate_title(content)
                encrypted_title = encryption_service.encrypt(title)
                repository.update_conversation_title(
                    conn=conn,
                    conversation_id=conversation_id,
                    user_email=user_email,
                    encrypted_title=encrypted_title,
                )

        return message_id

    
    # --------------------------------------------------
    # Update Message
    # --------------------------------------------------

    def update_message(
        self,
        conversation_id: str,
        user_email: str,
        message_id: str,
        content: str,
    ):

        # Verify conversation ownership.
        conversation = self.get_owned_conversation(
            conversation_id=conversation_id,
            user_email=user_email,
        )

        if not conversation:
            return False

        encrypted_content = encryption_service.encrypt(
            content
        )

        with get_pg_connection() as conn:

            updated = repository.update_message(
                conn=conn,
                message_id=message_id,
                conversation_id=conversation_id,
                encrypted_content=encrypted_content,
            )

        return updated
    
    
    # --------------------------------------------------
    # Get Messages
    # --------------------------------------------------

    def get_messages(
        self,
        conversation_id: str,
        user_email: str,
    ):

        # First verify conversation ownership.
        conversation = self.get_owned_conversation(
            conversation_id=conversation_id,
            user_email=user_email,
        )

        if not conversation:
            return None

        with get_pg_connection() as conn:

            messages = repository.get_messages(
                conn=conn,
                conversation_id=conversation_id,
            )

        return [
            self._decrypt_message(message)
            for message in messages
        ]


    # --------------------------------------------------
    # Delete Conversation
    # --------------------------------------------------

    def delete_conversation(
        self,
        conversation_id: str,
        user_email: str,
    ):

        with get_pg_connection() as conn:

            deleted = repository.delete_conversation(
                conn=conn,
                conversation_id=conversation_id,
                user_email=user_email,
            )

        return deleted


    # ==================================================
    # Internal Helpers
    # ==================================================

    def _decrypt_conversation(
        self,
        conversation: dict,
    ):

        conversation = dict(conversation)

        encrypted_title = conversation.get(
            "encrypted_title"
        )

        conversation["title"] = None

        if encrypted_title:
            try:
                conversation["title"] = (
                    encryption_service.decrypt(
                        encrypted_title
                    )
                )
            except Exception:
                # Don't expose encrypted DB values.
                conversation["title"] = None

        # Never expose encrypted storage value to API.
        conversation.pop(
            "encrypted_title",
            None,
        )

        return conversation


    def _decrypt_message(
        self,
        message: dict,
    ):

        message = dict(message)

        encrypted_content = message.get(
            "encrypted_content"
        )

        content = None

        if encrypted_content:
            try:
                content = encryption_service.decrypt(
                    encrypted_content
                )
            except Exception:
                content = None

        return {
            "id": str(message["id"]),
            "conversation_id": str(
                message["conversation_id"]
            ),
            "role": message["role"],
            "content": content,
            "message_type": message.get(
                "message_type",
                "chat",
            ),
            "created_at": message["created_at"],
        }

    
    def generate_title(self, content: str) -> str:
        cleaned = " ".join(
            content.strip().split()
        )

        max_length = 60

        if len(cleaned) <= max_length:
            return cleaned

        return cleaned[:max_length].rstrip() + "..."



########################################################################################################################
FILE: app/db/connection.py
FULL_PATH: /opt/AIDA/aida_react/backend/app/db/connection.py
########################################################################################################################

"""Postgres connection pool.

Exports two helpers:
- `get_pg_pool_conn()`: returns a raw pooled `PooledConnection` object.
  Callers must call `.close()` themselves (put it back in the pool).
  Used by service classes that manage the lifecycle explicitly.

- `get_pg_connection()`: context manager that acquires a connection from
  the pool and guarantees it is returned when the block exits.
  Use with `with get_pg_connection() as conn:`.
"""

import os
from dotenv import load_dotenv
from contextlib import contextmanager
from psycopg2 import pool
from app.utils.logger import setup_logger

logger = setup_logger()
load_dotenv()  # Load environment variables from .env file


class PooledConnection:
    """Wrap a pooled psycopg2 connection so `.close()` returns it to the pool."""

    def __init__(self, connection, pool_obj):
        self._connection = connection
        self._pool_obj = pool_obj
        self._closed = False

    def __getattr__(self, name):
        if self._closed:
            raise RuntimeError("Connection has already been released back to the pool.")
        return getattr(self._connection, name)

    def close(self):
        if not self._closed:
            self._pool_obj.putconn(self._connection)
            self._closed = True

    def __enter__(self):
        return self

    def __exit__(self, exc_type, exc, tb):
        self.close()
        return False


try:
    PG_POOL = pool.ThreadedConnectionPool(
        minconn=1,
        maxconn=20,
        host=os.getenv("DB_HOST"),
        port=os.getenv("DB_PORT"),
        dbname=os.getenv("DB_NAME"),
        user=os.getenv("DB_USER"),
        password=os.getenv("DB_PASSWORD"),
    )
    logger.info("Postgres connection pool created successfully.")
except Exception as e:
    logger.error("Failed to initialize Postgres connection pool: %s", e)
    raise


def get_pg_pool_conn():
    """Return a raw pooled connection compatible with existing call sites.

    Callers must call `.close()` to return the connection to the pool.
    Prefer the `get_pg_connection()` context manager when possible.
    """
    connection = PG_POOL.getconn()
    return PooledConnection(connection, PG_POOL)


@contextmanager
def get_pg_connection():
    """Context manager: acquire a Postgres connection from the pool.

    Ensures the connection is returned to the pool automatically after use.

    Usage::

        with get_pg_connection() as conn:
            ...
    """
    conn = get_pg_pool_conn()
    try:
        yield conn
    finally:
        conn.close()


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Back-compat aliases — remove once all call sites have been migrated.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
get_connection = get_pg_pool_conn          # was `get_connection`
get_db_connection = get_pg_connection      # was `get_db_connection`



########################################################################################################################
FILE: app/db/oracle_connection.py
FULL_PATH: /opt/AIDA/aida_react/backend/app/db/oracle_connection.py
########################################################################################################################

"""Oracle DB connection helper.

Exports `get_oracle_connection()` to clearly distinguish it from the
postgres pool in `app.db.connection` which exports `get_pg_connection()`.
"""

import os
import threading
import oracledb
from app.config.settings import ORACLE_DB_CONFIG
from app.utils.logger import setup_logger

logger = setup_logger()

_oracle_pool = None
_pool_lock = threading.Lock()
_oracle_client_initialized = False


def get_oracle_connection():
    """Acquire and return an Oracle DB connection from the shared session pool.

    Uses thick mode when an Instant Client path is configured and exists on disk;
    falls back to thin mode otherwise.
    The connection returned by `pool.acquire()` is compatible with existing `.close()` calls.
    """
    global _oracle_pool, _oracle_client_initialized

    if _oracle_pool is not None:
        return _oracle_pool.acquire()

    with _pool_lock:
        if _oracle_pool is None:
            lib_path = ORACLE_DB_CONFIG.get("path")
            if lib_path and os.path.exists(lib_path) and not _oracle_client_initialized:
                try:
                    oracledb.init_oracle_client(lib_dir=lib_path)
                    _oracle_client_initialized = True
                    logger.info("Oracle Instant Client initialized in thick mode.")
                except oracledb.ProgrammingError as e:
                    if "already initialized" in str(e).lower():
                        _oracle_client_initialized = True
                    else:
                        logger.error("Failed to initialize Oracle client in thick mode: %s", e)
                        raise

            try:
                # Create a session pool for Oracle
                _oracle_pool = oracledb.create_pool(
                    user=ORACLE_DB_CONFIG.get("user"),
                    password=ORACLE_DB_CONFIG.get("password"),
                    dsn=ORACLE_DB_CONFIG.get("dsn"),
                    min=1,
                    max=10,
                    increment=1,
                )
                logger.info("Oracle connection pool created successfully.")
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error("Failed to create Oracle connection pool: %s", e)
                raise

    return _oracle_pool.acquire()




########################################################################################################################
FILE: app/db/reporting_connection.py
FULL_PATH: /opt/AIDA/aida_react/backend/app/db/reporting_connection.py
########################################################################################################################

import os

import psycopg2
from dotenv import load_dotenv


load_dotenv()


def get_reporting_pg_connection(instance: str):
    """Connect to the Agosense Reporting PostgreSQL database."""

    instance = (instance or "").strip().upper()

    if instance not in {"PROD1", "PROD2", "QA2"}:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Unsupported Reporting DB instance: {instance!r}"
        )

    prefix = f"{instance}_DB"

    required_settings = {
        "host": f"{prefix}_HOST",
        "port": f"{prefix}_PORT",
        "dbname": f"{prefix}_NAME",
        "user": f"{prefix}_USER",
        "password": f"{prefix}_PASSWORD",
    }

    config = {}

    for key, env_name in required_settings.items():
        value = os.getenv(env_name)

        if not value:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"Missing required environment variable: {env_name}"
            )

        config[key] = value

    config["port"] = int(config["port"])

    return psycopg2.connect(**config)

FULL_PATH: /opt/AIDA/aida_react/backend/app/exchange_history/reporting_repository.py

def _row_to_dict(cursor, row):
    if row is None:
        return None

    columns = [column[0] for column in cursor.description]
    return dict(zip(columns, row))


def get_transaction_by_job_id(conn, job_id: str):
    cursor = conn.cursor()

    try:
        cursor.execute(
            """
            SELECT *
            FROM transactionbean
            WHERE jobid = %s
            ORDER BY startstamp DESC, id DESC
            LIMIT 1
            """,
            (job_id,),
        )

        return _row_to_dict(cursor, cursor.fetchone())

    finally:
        cursor.close()


def get_transaction_reports(conn, transaction_id):
    cursor = conn.cursor()

    try:
        cursor.execute(
            """
            SELECT *
            FROM transactionreportbean
            WHERE transaction_id = %s
            ORDER BY stamp ASC, id ASC
            """,
            (transaction_id,),
        )

        rows = cursor.fetchall()
        columns = [column[0] for column in cursor.description]

        return [
            dict(zip(columns, row))
            for row in rows
        ]

    finally:
        cursor.close()



########################################################################################################################
FILE: app/exchange_history/reporting_service.py
FULL_PATH: /opt/AIDA/aida_react/backend/app/exchange_history/reporting_service.py
########################################################################################################################

import html
import re

from app.db.reporting_connection import get_reporting_pg_connection
from app.utils.logger import setup_logger

from . import reporting_repository


logger = setup_logger()


def _clean_text(value):
    if value is None:
        return ""

    value = html.unescape(str(value))
    value = re.sub(r"<br\s*/?>", "\n", value, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
    value = re.sub(r"<[^>]+>", " ", value)
    return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", value).strip()


def _extract_scoped_count(reports):
    for report in reports:
        summary = _clean_text(report.get("summary", ""))
        description = _clean_text(report.get("description", ""))

        text = f"{summary} {description}"

        match = re.search(
            r"The process scoped\s*\[(\d+)\]\s*identifier",
            text,
            re.IGNORECASE,
        )

        if match:
            return int(match.group(1))

    return None


def _extract_identifier(text):
    # Example:
    # StringId{id='NGEN32023-31568' fileName='...'}
    match = re.search(
        r"StringId\s*\{\s*id\s*=\s*'([^']*)'",
        text,
        re.IGNORECASE,
    )

    if match:
        identifier = match.group(1).strip()
        return identifier if identifier.lower() != "null" else None

    return None


def _extract_failure_message(description):
    text = _clean_text(description)

    # Preserve the meaningful underlying error when available.
    marker = re.search(
        r"error while executing feature",
        text,
        re.IGNORECASE,
    )

    if marker:
        return text[marker.start():].strip()

    return text or "Execution failed. No description was provided."


class ReportingService:

    def get_trigger_result(self, job_id: str, instance: str):
        """
        Retrieve and interpret Agosense Reporting Logs for a job.

        Scope outcomes and execution failures are derived from
        transactionreportbean. Created/Updated outcomes are handled
        separately using Dashboard data.
        """

        conn = None

        try:
            conn = get_reporting_pg_connection(instance)

            transaction = (
                reporting_repository.get_transaction_by_job_id(
                    conn,
                    job_id,
                )
            )

            if not transaction:
                return {
                    "status": "NOT_FOUND",
                    "job_id": job_id,
                    "instance": instance,
                    "message": (
                        "No Reporting transaction was found for this job."
                    ),
                }

            transaction_id = transaction["id"]

            reports = (
                reporting_repository.get_transaction_reports(
                    conn,
                    transaction_id,
                )
            )

            scope_failed_report = next(
                (
                    report
                    for report in reports
                    if re.search(
                        r"\[SCOPE\]\s*failed",
                        _clean_text(report.get("summary", "")),
                        re.IGNORECASE,
                    )
                ),
                None,
            )


            # Case 1: Scoping failed.
            if scope_failed_report:
                return {
                    "status": "SCOPE_FAILED",
                    "job_id": job_id,
                    "transaction_id": transaction_id,
                    "scoped_count": None,
                    "scope_failure_message": _clean_text(
                        scope_failed_report.get("description")
                    ),
                    "failures": [],
                }

            scoped_count = _extract_scoped_count(reports)

            # No scope count found is not automatically success.
            if scoped_count is None:
                return {
                    "status": "SCOPE_RESULT_UNKNOWN",
                    "job_id": job_id,
                    "transaction_id": transaction_id,
                    "scoped_count": None,
                    "scope_failure_message": None,
                    "failures": [],
                    "message": (
                        "Reporting Logs did not contain a recognizable "
                        "scope success or scope failure event."
                    ),
                }

            # Case 2: Scope succeeded but no identifiers were found.
            if scoped_count == 0:
                return {
                    "status": "SCOPE_SUCCESS_ZERO",
                    "job_id": job_id,
                    "transaction_id": transaction_id,
                    "scoped_count": 0,
                    "scope_failure_message": None,
                    "failures": [],
                }

            # Case 3: Scope succeeded with identifiers.
            failures = []
            seen_identifiers = set()

            for report in reports:
                summary = _clean_text(report.get("summary", ""))

                if not re.search(
                    r"\[EXECUTE\]\s*failed",
                    summary,
                    re.IGNORECASE,
                ):
                    continue

                description = _clean_text(
                    report.get("description", "")
                )
                combined_text = f"{summary} {description}"

                identifier = _extract_identifier(combined_text)

                # Do not turn the null identifier from a zero-result
                # execution into a defect failure.
                if not identifier:
                    continue

                # Reporting may contain repeated failure records.
                if identifier in seen_identifiers:
                    continue

                seen_identifiers.add(identifier)

                failures.append({
                    "source_id": identifier,
                    "status": "FAILED",
                    "message": _extract_failure_message(description),
                    "timestamp": (
                        report.get("stamp").isoformat()
                        if report.get("stamp")
                        else None
                    ),
                })

            return {
                "status": "SCOPE_SUCCESS",
                "job_id": job_id,
                "transaction_id": transaction_id,
                "scoped_count": scoped_count,
                "scope_failure_message": None,
                "failures": failures,
            }

        except Exception:
            logger.exception(
                "Failed to retrieve Reporting Logs for job %s "
                "from instance %s",
                job_id,
                instance,
            )
            raise

        finally:
            if conn is not None:
                conn.close()

from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from collections import defaultdict
from app.utils.time_parser import extract_date_from_process_id


def get_exchange_history_for_defects_old(conn, defect_ids: list):
    final_output = {}
    cur = conn.cursor()

    try:
        for input_id in defect_ids:
            # --- STEP 1: Get 'Created' metadata ---
            query_meta = """
                SELECT sourcewiid, destinationwiid, processid
                FROM exchangedescription 
                WHERE (sourcewiid = :id OR destinationwiid = :id) 
                  AND status = 'Created'
                ORDER BY processid DESC
            """
            cur.execute(query_meta, {"id": input_id})
            meta_row = cur.fetchone()

            related_ids = [meta_row[0], meta_row[1]] if meta_row else [input_id]
            
            created_date = "N/A"
            if meta_row and len(meta_row[2]) >= 6:
                p_id = meta_row[2]
                created_date = f"{p_id[4:6]}-{p_id[2:4]}-20{p_id[0:2]}"

            # --- STEP 2: Fetch Top 5 History Rows ---
            placeholders = [f":v{i}" for i in range(len(related_ids))]
            history_sql = f"""
                SELECT * FROM (
                    SELECT ed.*, 
                    ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY ed.sourcewiid ORDER BY ed.processid DESC) as rank_per_id
                    FROM exchangedescription ed 
                    WHERE ed.sourcewiid IN ({",".join(placeholders)})
                ) WHERE rank_per_id <= 5
                ORDER BY processid DESC
            """
            
            bind_params = {f"v{i}": val for i, val in enumerate(related_ids)}
            cur.execute(history_sql, bind_params)
            
            cols = [col[0].lower() for col in cur.description]
            history_rows = [dict(zip(cols, row)) for row in cur.fetchall()]

            # --- STEP 3: Group by Direction ---
            # Using a dictionary to group rows by their direction string
            grouped_history = defaultdict(list)
            for row in history_rows:
                proc_id = str(row.get('processid', ''))
                direction = proc_id.split('_')[-1] if '_' in proc_id else "unknown"
                grouped_history[direction].append(row)
                row['processid'] = extract_date_from_process_id(proc_id)  # Convert processid to date format for output

            # --- STEP 4: Format the Grouped History ---
            formatted_history_list = []
            for direction, rows in grouped_history.items():
                # Since we ordered by processid DESC, the first row is the latest
                latest_row = rows[0]
                failed_message = ""
                if latest_row.get('status') == 'Failed':
                    failed_message = latest_row.get('description')
                
                formatted_history_list.append({
                    "direction": direction,
                    "latest_status": latest_row.get('status'),
                    "failed_message": failed_message,
                    "latest_timestamp": latest_row.get('processid'),
                    "raw_data": rows  # This now contains all 5 (or fewer) rows for this direction
                })

            # --- STEP 5: Final Object ---
            final_output[input_id] = {
                "Created": created_date,
                "sourceid": meta_row[0] if meta_row else input_id,
                "destinationid": meta_row[1] if meta_row else "N/A",
                "history": formatted_history_list
            }

        return final_output
    

    except Exception as e:
        import logging
        logging.getLogger("exchange_engine").error("Database error in get_exchange_history_for_defects_old: %s", e)
        return {}
    finally:
        cur.close()



def get_exchange_history_for_defects(conn, defect_ids: list):
    if not defect_ids:
        return {}

    final_output = {}
    cur = conn.cursor()
    
    try:
        # STEP 1: Batch fetch 'Created' metadata for ALL IDs at once
        placeholders = [f":id{i}" for i in range(len(defect_ids))]
        meta_sql = f"""
            SELECT sourcewiid, destinationwiid, processid
            FROM exchangedescription 
            WHERE (sourcewiid IN ({','.join(placeholders)}) 
               OR destinationwiid IN ({','.join(placeholders)}))
              AND status = 'Created'
        """
        # Map IDs to bind variables
        bind_meta = {f"id{i}": d_id for i, d_id in enumerate(defect_ids)}
        cur.execute(meta_sql, bind_meta)
        
        # Store relations in a lookup map
        # We use a set for related_ids to ensure uniqueness
        id_relations = {d_id: {d_id} for d_id in defect_ids}
        meta_data_map = {}

        for s_id, d_id, p_id in cur.fetchall():
            # Find which input_id this record belongs to
            for input_id in defect_ids:
                if s_id == input_id or d_id == input_id:
                    id_relations[input_id].add(s_id)
                    id_relations[input_id].add(d_id)
                    meta_data_map[input_id] = (s_id, d_id, p_id)

        # STEP 2: Collect every unique ID we need to look up in history
        all_unique_lookups = list({item for sublist in id_relations.values() for item in sublist})
        
        # STEP 3: Batch fetch Top 5 History for ALL unique IDs in ONE query
        h_placeholders = [f":h{i}" for i in range(len(all_unique_lookups))]
        history_sql = f"""
            SELECT * FROM (
                SELECT ed.*, 
                ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY ed.sourcewiid ORDER BY ed.processid DESC) as rank_per_id
                FROM exchangedescription ed 
                WHERE ed.sourcewiid IN ({",".join(h_placeholders)})
            ) WHERE rank_per_id <= 5
        """
        bind_history = {f"h{i}": val for i, val in enumerate(all_unique_lookups)}
        cur.execute(history_sql, bind_history)
        
        cols = [col[0].lower() for col in cur.description]
        all_raw_rows = [dict(zip(cols, row)) for row in cur.fetchall()]

        # STEP 4: Reconstruct the results in Python (No more DB calls)
        for input_id in defect_ids:
            related_set = id_relations[input_id]
            meta = meta_data_map.get(input_id)
            
            # Filter history rows belonging to this specific input_id group
            relevant_history = [r for r in all_raw_rows if r['sourcewiid'] in related_set]
            
            # Group by direction
            grouped_history = defaultdict(list)
            for row in relevant_history:
                direction = str(row['processid']).split('_')[-1] if '_' in str(row['processid']) else "unknown"
                grouped_history[direction].append(row)
                
                raw_process_id = str(row['processid'] or '')
                row['execution_id'] = raw_process_id  # Keep the original processid for reference
                row['processid'] = extract_date_from_process_id(raw_process_id)  # Convert processid to date format for output

            # Format history list
            formatted_history = []
            for direction, rows in grouped_history.items():
                latest = rows[0]
                failed_message = ""
                if latest.get('status') == 'Failed':
                    failed_message = latest.get('description')
                formatted_history.append({
                    "direction": direction,
                    "latest_status": latest.get('status'),
                    "failed_message": failed_message,
                    "latest_timestamp": latest.get('processid'),
                    "raw_data": rows
                })

            # Extract date for the 'Created' field
            p_id_created = meta[2] if meta else "N/A"
            created_fmt = "N/A"
            if p_id_created != "N/A" and len(p_id_created) >= 6:
                created_fmt = f"{p_id_created[4:6]}-{p_id_created[2:4]}-20{p_id_created[0:2]}"

            final_output[input_id] = {
                "Created": created_fmt,
                "sourceid": meta[0] if meta else input_id,
                "destinationid": meta[1] if meta else "N/A",
                "history": formatted_history
            }

        return final_output

    finally:
        cur.close()



def get_failed_defects(
    conn,
    project_key=None,
    start_time=None,
    end_time=None,
    direction=None,
    customer=None,
    project=None,
    process_name=None,
):
    inner = """
        SELECT
            ed.sourcewiid,
            ed.destinationwiid,
            ed.status,
            ed.description,
            ed.processid
        FROM exchangedescription ed
        WHERE UPPER(ed.status) = 'FAILED'
    """

    params = {}

    # Preserve the existing date-window behavior.
    inner += """
        AND TO_DATE(
            SUBSTR(ed.processid, 1, 12),
            'YYMMDDHH24MISS'
        ) BETWEEN :start_dt AND :end_dt
    """

    today = datetime.now()
    today_start = today.replace(
        hour=0, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0
    )
    params["start_dt"] = today_start - timedelta(days=1)
    params["end_dt"] = today

    # Prefer configured Dashboard process identity when supplied.
    if customer and project and process_name:
        inner += """
            AND EXISTS (
                SELECT 1
                FROM exchangeprocess ep
                WHERE ep.processid = ed.processid
                  AND UPPER(TRIM(ep.customername)) = UPPER(TRIM(:customer))
                  AND UPPER(TRIM(ep.projectname)) = UPPER(TRIM(:project))
                  AND UPPER(TRIM(ep.processname)) = UPPER(TRIM(:process_name))
            )
        """
        params["customer"] = customer
        params["project"] = project
        params["process_name"] = process_name

    elif project_key:
        # Backward compatibility for existing callers.
        inner += """
            AND (
                UPPER(ed.sourcewiid) LIKE :src
                OR UPPER(ed.destinationwiid) LIKE :dest
            )
        """
        prefix = f"{project_key.upper()}-%"
        params["src"] = prefix
        params["dest"] = prefix

    if direction:
        inner += " AND UPPER(ed.processid) LIKE :direction"
        params["direction"] = f"%{direction.upper()}%"

    inner += """
        ORDER BY TO_DATE(
            SUBSTR(ed.processid, 1, 12),
            'YYMMDDHH24MISS'
        ) DESC
    """

    query = f"""
        SELECT *
        FROM ({inner})
        WHERE ROWNUM <= 100
    """
    cur = conn.cursor()
    try:
        cur.execute(query, params)
        return cur.fetchall()
    finally:
        cur.close()



def get_all_failures(conn):
    cur = conn.cursor()
    cur.execute("""
        SELECT sourcewiid, destinationwiid, status, description, processid
        FROM exchangedescription
        WHERE status = 'Failed'
    """)
    rows = cur.fetchall()
    cur.close()
    return rows



def get_trigger_sync_result(conn, customer, project, process_name):
    cur = conn.cursor()

    try:
        # ---------------------------------------------------------
        # STEP 1:
        # Get the latest processid from exchangeprocess
        # ---------------------------------------------------------
        process_query = """
            SELECT processid
            FROM (
                SELECT processid
                FROM exchangeprocess
                WHERE customername = :customer
                  AND projectname = :project
                  AND processname = :process_name
                ORDER BY processid DESC
            )
            WHERE ROWNUM = 1
        """

        cur.execute(
            process_query,
            {
                "customer": customer,
                "project": project,
                "process_name": process_name,
            }
        )

        process_row = cur.fetchone()

        print(f"Query executed for latest processid with customer={customer}, project={project}, process_name={process_name} and result: {process_row}")

        if not process_row:
            return None

        process_id = process_row[0]

        print(f"Latest Dashboard processid found: {process_id}")

        # ---------------------------------------------------------
        # STEP 2:
        # Get exchangedescription for that processid
        # ---------------------------------------------------------
        description_query = """
            SELECT *
            FROM exchangedescription
            WHERE processid = :process_id
        """

        cur.execute(
            description_query,
            {
                "process_id": process_id
            }
        )

        rows = cur.fetchall()

        if not rows:
            return []

        # Convert Oracle column names to lowercase keys
        columns = [
            column[0].lower()
            for column in cur.description
        ]

        result = [
            dict(zip(columns, row))
            for row in rows
        ]

        return result

    finally:
        cur.close()





def get_execution_context(conn, execution_id: str):
    """
    Retrieve one Oracle exchange execution and all its defect records.
    execution_id is exchangeprocess.PROCESSID, not processes.id.
    """
    cur = conn.cursor()

    try:
        cur.execute("""
            SELECT
                processid,
                customername,
                projectname,
                processname,
                startdate,
                starttime,
                endtime,
                overallstatus,
                total,
                satisfied,
                updated,
                created,
                failed
            FROM exchangeprocess
            WHERE processid = :process_id
        """, {"process_id": execution_id})

        process_row = cur.fetchone()

        if not process_row:
            return None

        process_columns = [
            column[0].lower()
            for column in cur.description
        ]
        process_data = dict(zip(process_columns, process_row))

        cur.execute("""
            SELECT
                descriptionid,
                status,
                sourcewiid,
                destinationwiid,
                description,
                processid
            FROM exchangedescription
            WHERE processid = :process_id
            ORDER BY descriptionid
        """, {"process_id": execution_id})

        description_columns = [
            column[0].lower()
            for column in cur.description
        ]

        records = [
            dict(zip(description_columns, row))
            for row in cur.fetchall()
        ]

        failed_records = [
            record
            for record in records
            if str(record.get("status", "")).strip().upper() == "FAILED"
        ]

        return {
            "execution": process_data,
            "records": records,
            "failed_records": failed_records
        }

    finally:
        cur.close()



########################################################################################################################
FILE: app/exchange_history/result_reconciler.py
FULL_PATH: /opt/AIDA/aida_react/backend/app/exchange_history/result_reconciler.py
########################################################################################################################

from collections import defaultdict
from typing import Any


def _normalize_id(value: Any) -> str | None:
    if value is None:
        return None

    value = str(value).strip()

    if not value or value.lower() in {"null", "none"}:
        return None

    return value


def _id_key(value: str) -> str:
    return value.casefold()


def _dashboard_operation(row: dict) -> str:
    """
    Determine Created vs Updated from the operation description first.
    The Dashboard status field alone is not reliable for this distinction.
    """
    description = str(row.get("description") or "").strip().lower()
    status = str(row.get("status") or "").strip().upper()

    if status == "FAILED" or "failed" in description:
        return "FAILED"

    if "created successfully" in description:
        return "CREATED"

    if "updated successfully" in description:
        return "UPDATED"

    if status == "CREATED":
        return "CREATED"

    if status == "UPDATED":
        return "UPDATED"

    return "OUTCOME_UNKNOWN"


def reconcile_trigger_results(
    reporting_result: dict | None,
    dashboard_result: dict | list | None,
) -> dict:
    """
    Merge Reporting Logs failures and Dashboard exchange records into
    one result per source defect ID.

    Reporting Logs:
      - Scope status and scoped count
      - Detailed execution failure messages

    Dashboard:
      - Created/Updated outcome
      - Destination defect ID
      - Dashboard failure description
    """
    reporting_result = reporting_result or {}
    reporting_status = reporting_result.get("status", "LOOKUP_FAILED")

    # Do not reconcile stale Dashboard records for a scope failure.
    if reporting_status == "SCOPE_FAILED":
        return {
            "status": "SCOPE_FAILED",
            "scoped_count": None,
            "success_count": 0,
            "failure_count": 0,
            "unknown_count": 0,
            "unaccounted_scoped_count": None,
            "results": [],
            "scope_failure_message": reporting_result.get(
                "scope_failure_message"
            ),
            "message": "The process failed during scoping.",
        }

    if reporting_status == "SCOPE_SUCCESS_ZERO":
        return {
            "status": "NO_DEFECTS_SCOPED",
            "scoped_count": 0,
            "success_count": 0,
            "failure_count": 0,
            "unknown_count": 0,
            "unaccounted_scoped_count": 0,
            "results": [],
            "scope_failure_message": None,
            "message": "Scoping succeeded, but no defects matched.",
        }

    # Accept either the wrapped Dashboard structure or the original list.
    if isinstance(dashboard_result, dict):
        dashboard_records = dashboard_result.get("records") or []
        dashboard_lookup_status = dashboard_result.get("status")
    elif isinstance(dashboard_result, list):
        dashboard_records = dashboard_result
        dashboard_lookup_status = (
            "RETRIEVED" if dashboard_records else "NO_RECORDS"
        )
    else:
        dashboard_records = []
        dashboard_lookup_status = None

    # Store results by normalized source ID to avoid duplicates.
    merged: dict[str, dict] = {}

    for row in dashboard_records:
        source_id = _normalize_id(
            row.get("sourcewiid") or row.get("source_id")
        )

        # Ignore rows that cannot be matched to a source defect.
        if not source_id:
            continue

        key = _id_key(source_id)
        operation = _dashboard_operation(row)

        destination_id = _normalize_id(
            row.get("destinationwiid") or row.get("destination_id")
        )

        description = str(row.get("description") or "").strip()

        if key not in merged:
            merged[key] = {
                "source_id": source_id,
                "destination_id": destination_id,
                "status": operation,
                "message": description or None,
                "dashboard_status": row.get("status"),
                "reporting_failure": False,
            }
        else:
            # Preserve a destination ID if a later record provides one.
            if destination_id:
                merged[key]["destination_id"] = destination_id

            # A failure must not be overwritten by a success record.
            if operation == "FAILED":
                merged[key]["status"] = "FAILED"
                merged[key]["message"] = description or merged[key]["message"]

    # Index Reporting Logs failures by source ID.
    reporting_failures: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)

    for failure in reporting_result.get("failures") or []:
        source_id = _normalize_id(
            failure.get("source_id") or failure.get("sourcewiid")
        )

        if not source_id:
            continue

        message = str(failure.get("message") or "").strip()
        if message:
            key = _id_key(source_id)
            if message not in reporting_failures[key]:
                reporting_failures[key].append(message)

        key = _id_key(source_id)

        if key not in merged:
            merged[key] = {
                "source_id": source_id,
                "destination_id": None,
                "status": "FAILED",
                "message": message or "Execution failed.",
                "dashboard_status": None,
                "reporting_failure": True,
            }
        else:
            # Reporting Logs is authoritative for execution failure details.
            merged[key]["status"] = "FAILED"
            merged[key]["reporting_failure"] = True

            messages = reporting_failures[key]
            if messages:
                merged[key]["message"] = "\n".join(messages)

    results = list(merged.values())
    results.sort(key=lambda item: item["source_id"].casefold())

    success_count = sum(
        item["status"] in {"CREATED", "UPDATED"}
        for item in results
    )
    failure_count = sum(
        item["status"] == "FAILED"
        for item in results
    )
    unknown_count = sum(
        item["status"] == "OUTCOME_UNKNOWN"
        for item in results
    )

    scoped_count = reporting_result.get("scoped_count")

    # A scoped defect without a matched result has no known per-defect outcome.
    unaccounted_scoped_count = None
    if isinstance(scoped_count, int):
        unaccounted_scoped_count = max(
            scoped_count - len(results),
            0,
        )

    # Determine the overall outcome without inferring success from missing data.
    if reporting_status in {"LOOKUP_FAILED", "NOT_FOUND"}:
        overall_status = "REPORTING_LOOKUP_FAILED"

    elif reporting_status == "SCOPE_RESULT_UNKNOWN":
        overall_status = "SCOPE_RESULT_UNKNOWN"

    elif (
        dashboard_lookup_status in {"LOOKUP_FAILED", "NOT_RETRIEVED"}
        or unaccounted_scoped_count is None
        or unaccounted_scoped_count > 0
        or unknown_count > 0
    ):
        overall_status = "OUTCOME_INCOMPLETE"

    elif failure_count and success_count:
        overall_status = "PARTIAL_FAILURE"

    elif failure_count:
        overall_status = "FAILED"

    elif success_count:
        overall_status = "SUCCESS"

    else:
        overall_status = "OUTCOME_INCOMPLETE"

    return {
        "status": overall_status,
        "scoped_count": scoped_count,
        "success_count": success_count,
        "failure_count": failure_count,
        "unknown_count": unknown_count,
        "unaccounted_scoped_count": unaccounted_scoped_count,
        "results": results,
        "scope_failure_message": reporting_result.get(
            "scope_failure_message"
        ),
        "message": (
            "Some scoped defects do not have a verifiable outcome."
            if overall_status == "OUTCOME_INCOMPLETE"
            else None
        ),
    }



########################################################################################################################

"""Exchange history service.

Queries Oracle DB for defect sync history, failure summaries, and top
failure reasons.  All DB access goes through `get_oracle_connection()`.
"""

from app.db.oracle_connection import get_oracle_connection
from app.utils.logger import setup_logger
from . import repository
from app.utils.defect_parser import extract_defect_ids
from app.utils.time_parser import extract_time_range
from collections import defaultdict
from datetime import datetime
import re

logger = setup_logger()


class ExchangeHistoryService:

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Defect History
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def get_history(self, query_text: str):
        defect_ids = extract_defect_ids(query_text)
        _, _ = extract_time_range(query_text)  # parsed but not used here yet

        if not defect_ids:
            return {
                "status": "clarification_required",
                "message": "Please provide at least one defect ID.",
            }

        conn = get_oracle_connection()
        try:
            rows = repository.get_exchange_history_for_defects(conn, defect_ids)
        finally:
            conn.close()

        if not rows:
            return {"status": "not_found", "defects": defect_ids}

        return {"status": "success", "results": rows}

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Failure Summary
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def get_failure_summary(self, query_text: str, project_key: str):

        # --- Extract direction ---
        direction_match = re.search(r"\b(\w+to\w+)\b", query_text)
        direction = direction_match.group(1) if direction_match else None

        # --- Extract time range ---
        start_time, end_time = extract_time_range(query_text)

        conn = get_oracle_connection()
        try:
            rows = repository.get_failed_defects(
                conn,
                project_key=project_key,
                direction=direction,
            )
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error("DB error in get_failed_defects: %s", e)
            raise
        finally:
            conn.close()

        results = []
        unique_defects: set = set()
        directions_seen: set = set()

        for row in rows:
            source_id, dest_id, status, desc, processid = row

            timestamp_part, dir_value = processid.split("_", 1)
            dt = datetime.strptime(timestamp_part, "%y%m%d%H%M%S")

            # --- Apply time filter ---
            if start_time and end_time:
                if not (start_time <= dt <= end_time):
                    continue

            defect_id = source_id if source_id else dest_id

            results.append(
                {
                    "defect_id": defect_id,
                    "timestamp": dt.isoformat(),
                    "direction": dir_value,
                    "message": desc,
                }
            )

            unique_defects.add(defect_id)
            directions_seen.add(dir_value)

        # Sort once, AFTER the loop — not inside it
        results.sort(key=lambda x: x["timestamp"], reverse=True)

        page = 1
        page_size = 20
        start_index = (page - 1) * page_size
        end_index = start_index + page_size
        paginated_results = results[start_index:end_index]

        return {
            "status": "success",
            "filters_applied": {
                "project_key": project_key,
                "direction": direction,
                "time_range": {
                    "start": start_time.isoformat() if start_time else None,
                    "end": end_time.isoformat() if end_time else None,
                },
            },
            "summary": {
                "total_failures": len(results),
                "unique_defects": len(unique_defects),
                "directions_involved": list(directions_seen),
            },
            "failures": paginated_results,
        }

    def get_failure_summary_direct(self, project_key: str, direction: str = None, hours: int = 24, customer: str = None, project: str = None, process_name: str = None):
        """Clean method that takes direct parameters instead of regex parsing a query text."""
        from datetime import timedelta
        now = datetime.utcnow()
        start_time = now - timedelta(hours=hours)
        end_time = now
        
        conn = get_oracle_connection()
        try:
            rows = repository.get_failed_defects(
                conn,
                project_key=project_key,
                direction=direction,
                start_time=start_time,
                end_time=end_time,
                customer=customer,
                project=project,
                process_name=process_name
            )
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error("DB error in get_failed_defects: %s", e)
            raise
        finally:
            conn.close()

        results = []
        unique_defects: set = set()
        directions_seen: set = set()

        for row in rows:
            source_id, dest_id, status, desc, processid = row

            timestamp_part, dir_value = processid.split("_", 1)
            dt = datetime.strptime(timestamp_part, "%y%m%d%H%M%S")

            # Apply time filter
            if start_time and end_time:
                if not (start_time <= dt <= end_time):
                    continue

            defect_id = source_id if source_id else dest_id

            results.append(
                {
                    "defect_id": defect_id,
                    "timestamp": dt.isoformat(),
                    "direction": dir_value,
                    "message": desc,
                }
            )

            unique_defects.add(defect_id)
            directions_seen.add(dir_value)

        results.sort(key=lambda x: x["timestamp"], reverse=True)

        page = 1
        page_size = 20
        start_index = (page - 1) * page_size
        end_index = start_index + page_size
        paginated_results = results[start_index:end_index]

        return {
            "status": "success",
            "filters_applied": {
                "project_key": project_key,
                "direction": direction,
                "time_range": {
                    "start": start_time.isoformat() if start_time else None,
                    "end": end_time.isoformat() if end_time else None,
                },
            },
            "summary": {
                "total_failures": len(results),
                "unique_defects": len(unique_defects),
                "directions_involved": list(directions_seen),
            },
            "failures": paginated_results,
        }

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Top Failure Reasons
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def get_top_failure_reasons(self, query_text: str):
        query_upper = query_text.upper()

        # --- Extract project ---
        project_match = re.search(r"for\s+([A-Z0-9]+)", query_upper)
        project_key = project_match.group(1) if project_match else None

        # --- Extract direction ---
        direction_match = re.search(r"\b(\w+to\w+)\b", query_text)
        direction = direction_match.group(1) if direction_match else None

        # --- Extract time ---
        start_time, end_time = extract_time_range(query_text)

        conn = get_oracle_connection()
        try:
            rows = repository.get_failed_defects(
                conn,
                project_key=project_key,
                direction=direction,
            )
        finally:
            conn.close()

        reason_counter: dict = defaultdict(int)
        total_failures = 0

        for row in rows:
            source_id, dest_id, status, desc, processid = row

            timestamp_part, _ = processid.split("_", 1)
            dt = datetime.strptime(timestamp_part, "%y%m%d%H%M%S")

            # Apply time filter
            if start_time and end_time:
                if not (start_time <= dt <= end_time):
                    continue

            reason_counter[desc] += 1
            total_failures += 1

        sorted_reasons = sorted(
            reason_counter.items(),
            key=lambda x: x[1],
            reverse=True,
        )

        top_n = 5
        top_reasons = [
            {"reason": reason, "count": count}
            for reason, count in sorted_reasons[:top_n]
        ]

        return {
            "status": "success",
            "filters_applied": {
                "project_key": project_key,
                "direction": direction,
                "time_range": {
                    "start": start_time.isoformat() if start_time else None,
                    "end": end_time.isoformat() if end_time else None,
                },
            },
            "summary": {
                "total_failures": total_failures,
                "unique_reasons": len(reason_counter),
            },
            "top_failure_reasons": top_reasons,
        }

    def get_top_failure_reasons_direct(self, project_key: str, direction: str = None, hours: int = 24, customer: str = None, project: str = None, process_name: str = None):
        """Clean method that takes direct parameters instead of regex parsing a query text."""
        from datetime import timedelta
        now = datetime.utcnow()
        start_time = now - timedelta(hours=hours)
        end_time = now

        conn = get_oracle_connection()
        try:
            rows = repository.get_failed_defects(
                conn,
                project_key=project_key,
                direction=direction,
                start_time=start_time,
                end_time=end_time,
                customer=customer,
                project=project,
                process_name=process_name
            )
        finally:
            conn.close()

        reason_counter: dict = defaultdict(int)
        total_failures = 0

        for row in rows:
            source_id, dest_id, status, desc, processid = row

            timestamp_part, _ = processid.split("_", 1)
            dt = datetime.strptime(timestamp_part, "%y%m%d%H%M%S")

            # Apply time filter
            if start_time and end_time:
                if not (start_time <= dt <= end_time):
                    continue

            reason_counter[desc] += 1
            total_failures += 1

        sorted_reasons = sorted(
            reason_counter.items(),
            key=lambda x: x[1],
            reverse=True,
        )

        top_n = 5
        top_reasons = [
            {"reason": reason, "count": count}
            for reason, count in sorted_reasons[:top_n]
        ]

        return {
            "status": "success",
            "filters_applied": {
                "project_key": project_key,
                "direction": direction,
                "time_range": {
                    "start": start_time.isoformat() if start_time else None,
                    "end": end_time.isoformat() if end_time else None,
                },
            },
            "summary": {
                "total_failures": total_failures,
                "unique_reasons": len(reason_counter),
            },
            "top_failure_reasons": top_reasons,
        }

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Global Health
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def get_global_health(self):
        from datetime import timedelta

        now = datetime.utcnow()
        last_1_days = now - timedelta(days=1)
        previous_1_days = last_1_days - timedelta(days=1)

        conn = get_oracle_connection()
        try:
            rows = repository.get_all_failures(conn)
        finally:
            conn.close()

        total_today = 0
        total_last_1 = 0
        total_previous_1 = 0
        direction_counter: dict = {}

        for row in rows:
            source_id, dest_id, status, desc, processid = row

            timestamp_part, direction = processid.split("_", 1)
            dt = datetime.strptime(timestamp_part, "%y%m%d%H%M%S")

            if dt.date() == now.date():
                total_today += 1

            if last_1_days <= dt <= now:
                total_last_1 += 1

            if previous_1_days <= dt < last_1_days:
                total_previous_1 += 1

            direction_counter[direction] = direction_counter.get(direction, 0) + 1

        trend = None
        if total_previous_1 > 0:
            trend = round(
                ((total_last_1 - total_previous_1) / total_previous_1) * 100, 2
            )

        return {
            "summary": {
                "failures_today": total_today,
                "failures_last_1_days": total_last_1,
                "failures_previous_1_days": total_previous_1,
                "trend_percentage": trend,
                "direction_distribution": direction_counter,
            }
        }




    def get_trigger_sync_result(
        self,
        customer,
        project,
        process_name
    ):
        conn = get_oracle_connection()

        try:
            return repository.get_trigger_sync_result(
                conn,
                customer=customer,
                project=project,
                process_name=process_name,
            )

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(
                "DB error while fetching Trigger Sync result: %s",
                e,
            )
            raise

        finally:
            conn.close()


    
    def get_execution_context(self, execution_id: str):
        """
        Retrieve execution metadata and defect-level results
        from Oracle for an exact execution ID.
        """
        if not execution_id:
            return {
                "status": "invalid_request",
                "message": "Execution ID is required."
            }

        conn = get_oracle_connection()

        try:
            result = repository.get_execution_context(
                conn,
                execution_id
            )

            if not result:
                return {
                    "status": "not_found",
                    "execution_id": execution_id,
                    "message": "Exchange execution was not found."
                }

            return {
                "status": "success",
                "execution_id": execution_id,
                **result
            }

        except Exception:
            logger.exception(
                "Failed to retrieve exchange execution %s",
                execution_id
            )
            raise

        finally:
            conn.close()
