@app.post("/process/start")
async def start_process(
    request: ProcessRequest,
    aida_session: str | None = Cookie(
        default=None,
        alias="AIDA_SESSION",
    ),
):
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. Authenticate user from AIDA_SESSION
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------

    if not aida_session:
        return JSONResponse(
            status_code=401,
            content={
                "status": "error",
                "message": "Authentication required.",
            },
        )

    user_session = user_session_service.validate_session(
        aida_session
    )

    if not user_session:
        return JSONResponse(
            status_code=401,
            content={
                "status": "error",
                "message": "Invalid or expired AIDA session.",
            },
        )

    email = user_session["email"]

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. Resolve project ID -> project key
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------

    project = config_service.get_project_by_id(
        request.projectId
    )

    if not project:
        return JSONResponse(
            status_code=404,
            content={
                "status": "error",
                "message": (
                    f"Project {request.projectId} "
                    "was not found."
                ),
            },
        )

    project_key = project["project_key"]

    print(
        f"Trigger Sync authorization check: "
        f"email={email}, "
        f"project_key={project_key}"
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # 3. Trigger Sync authorization
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------

    authorization = (
        trigger_sync_access_service.authorize(
            email=email,
            project_key=project_key,
        )
    )

    if not authorization["allowed"]:

        contact_users = (
            trigger_sync_access_service.get_contact_users(
                project_key
            )
        )

        if contact_users:
            contacts_text = "\n".join(
                f"- {email}"
                for email in contact_users
            )

            message = (
                f"You do not have permission to trigger sync "
                f"for project {project_key}.\n\n"
                f"Please reach out to one of the following users "
                f"who have Trigger Sync access:\n"
                f"{contacts_text}"
            )
        else:
            message = (
                f"You do not have permission to trigger sync "
                f"for project {project_key}.\n\n"
                f"No Trigger Sync contacts are currently "
                f"configured for this project."
            )

        return JSONResponse(
            status_code=403,
            content={
                "status": "not_authorized",
                "message":message,
            },
        )

    print(
        f"Trigger Sync authorized: "
        f"email={email}, "
        f"project_key={project_key}, "
        f"role={authorization['role']}"
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # 4. Existing Trigger Sync logic
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------

    job_id = str(uuid4())

    print(
        f"Starting process with job_id: {job_id} "
        f"for OEM: {request.oemId}, "
        f"Project: {request.projectId}, "
        f"Process: {request.process}, "
        f"Defect IDs: {request.defectIds}"
    )

    validate_response = process_service.validate_process(
        request.oemId,
        request.projectId,
        request.process,
        request.defectIds,
    )

    print(
        "Validation response:",
        validate_response,
    )

    if validate_response.get("status_code") != 200:
        return JSONResponse(
            status_code=validate_response.get(
                "status_code",
                500,
            ),
            content={
                "status": "error",
                "message": validate_response.get(
                    "msg",
                    "Process validation failed.",
                ),
            },
        )

    


    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # 5. Create Trigger Sync audit record
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------

    source = (
        "quick_action"
        if request.chat_conversation_id
        else "process_start"
    )

    try:
        trigger_sync_audit_service.create_audit(
            job_id=job_id,
            triggered_by=email,
            source=source,
            oem=project.get("oem_name") or request.oemId,
            project_key=project_key,
            process_name=request.process.get("process_name")
            or request.process.get("name")
            or request.process.get("processName"),
            defect_ids=request.defectIds,
        )

    except Exception:
        logger.exception(
            "Unable to create Trigger Sync audit for job %s",
            job_id,
        )

        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=500,
            detail="Unable to record Trigger Sync request. Please try again.",
        )
    
    
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # 6. Persist Trigger Sync messages
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------

    trigger_message_id = None

    print("Trigger Sync Chat conversation ID:", request.chat_conversation_id)
    if request.chat_conversation_id:

        print(f"Adding message to conversation: {request.chat_conversation_id}")
        user_message = (
            f"Trigger Sync requested for "
            f"{project_key} / {request.process.get('name', 'Process')}\n\n"
            f"Defects: {request.defectIds}"
        )

        conversation_service.add_message(
            conversation_id=request.chat_conversation_id,
            user_email=email,
            role="user",
            content=user_message,
            message_type="quick_action_trigger",
        )

        assistant_message = (
            "### 🔄 Trigger Sync\n\n"
            "✅ **Sync triggered successfully.**\n\n"
            f"**Job ID:** `{job_id}`\n\n"
            f"**Defects:** {request.defectIds}\n\n"
            "Live progress updates will appear below."
        )

        trigger_message_id = conversation_service.add_message(
            conversation_id=request.chat_conversation_id,
            user_email=email,
            role="assistant",
            content=assistant_message,
            message_type="quick_action_trigger",
        )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # 7. Start existing process
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------

    try:
        await process_service.start_process(
            job_id,
            request.oemId,
            request.projectId,
            request.process,
            request.defectIds,
        )
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.exception(
            "Failed to start Trigger sync job %s: %s",
            job_id,
            exc,
        )

        trigger_sync_audit_service.mark_failed(
            job_id,
            str(exc)
        )
        
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=500,
            detail="Failed to start process. Please try again.",
        )

    return {
        "job_id": job_id,
        "trigger_message_id": trigger_message_id,
    }

    

@app.post("/feedback")
def store_feedback(
    session_id: str = Body(...),
    feedback: Optional[str] = Body(None),
    comment: Optional[str] = Body(None),):

    if not feedback and not comment:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail="Either feedback or comment must be provided.",
        )

    from app.monitoring import update_conversation_feedback
    update_conversation_feedback(session_id, feedback, comment)
    return {"status": "success"}

@app.websocket("/ws/{job_id}")
async def websocket_endpoint(websocket: WebSocket, job_id: str):
    await manager.connect(job_id, websocket)
    from app.exchange_trigger.process_store import jobs
    if job_id in jobs:
        try:
            await websocket.send_json(jobs[job_id])
        except Exception:
            pass
    try:
        while True:
            await websocket.receive_text()
    except WebSocketDisconnect:
        manager.disconnect(job_id, websocket)



@app.on_event("startup")
def startup_db_init():
    try:
        from app.monitoring.schema import init_db
        from app.monitoring.trigger_sync_schema import (
            init_trigger_sync_audit_db,
        )

        init_db()
        init_trigger_sync_audit_db()

    except Exception as exc:
        logger.error(
            "Failed to initialize monitoring database: %s",
            exc,
        )
        raise

# ── 3. Load DB Telemetry Records ─────────────────────────────────────────────
def load_data():
    try:
        with get_pg_connection() as conn:
            query = """
                SELECT 
                    id, 
                    session_id, 
                    question, 
                    response_given, 
                    feedback, 
                    comment, 
                    input_tokens, 
                    output_tokens, 
                    total_tokens, 
                    tool_calls_count,
                    tool_used, 
                    oem,
                    project_key,
                    process_name,
                    created_at 
                FROM conversations
                ORDER BY created_at DESC;
            """
            df = pd.read_sql(query, conn)
            if not df.empty:
                df['created_at'] = pd.to_datetime(df['created_at'])
            return df
    except Exception as e:
        st.error(f"Error loading database records: {e}")
        return pd.DataFrame()

df = load_data()

if df.empty:
    st.info("No telemetry logs found in the database. Start chat sessions to populate.")
else:
    # Fill N/As for context columns to allow clean sorting
    df['oem'] = df['oem'].fillna("N/A")
    df['project_key'] = df['project_key'].fillna("N/A")
    df['process_name'] = df['process_name'].fillna("N/A")

    # ── 4. Sidebar Hierarchical Filters ──────────────────────────────────────
    
    # 1. OEM Dropdown
    oems = sorted(list(df['oem'].unique()))
    selected_oem = st.sidebar.selectbox("Filter OEM", ["All"] + oems)
    
    # 2. Project Dropdown (Hierarchical - nested inside OEM)
    if selected_oem != "All":
        oem_filtered_df = df[df['oem'] == selected_oem]
    else:
        oem_filtered_df = df
        
    projects = sorted(list(oem_filtered_df['project_key'].unique()))
    selected_project = st.sidebar.selectbox("Filter Project", ["All"] + projects)
    
    # 3. Process Dropdown (Hierarchical - nested inside OEM + Project)
    if selected_project != "All":
        project_filtered_df = oem_filtered_df[oem_filtered_df['project_key'] == selected_project]
    else:
        project_filtered_df = oem_filtered_df
        
    processes = sorted(list(project_filtered_df['process_name'].unique()))
    selected_process = st.sidebar.selectbox("Filter Process", ["All"] + processes)
    
    # Apply context filters
    context_filtered_df = project_filtered_df
    if selected_process != "All":
        context_filtered_df = context_filtered_df[context_filtered_df['process_name'] == selected_process]

    # 4. Date selection (Placed at bottom of sidebar so calendar picker opens upwards, preventing cutoff)
    if not context_filtered_df.empty:
        min_date = context_filtered_df['created_at'].min().date()
        max_date = context_filtered_df['created_at'].max().date()
    else:
        min_date = datetime.now().date()
        max_date = datetime.now().date()
        
    date_range = st.sidebar.date_input(
        "Date Range",
        [min_date, max_date],
        min_value=min_date,
        max_value=max_date
    )
    
    start_date = date_range[0] if len(date_range) > 0 else min_date
    end_date = date_range[1] if len(date_range) > 1 else max_date
    
    # Apply final filters (Context + Date)
    filtered_df = context_filtered_df[
        (context_filtered_df['created_at'].dt.date >= start_date) & 
        (context_filtered_df['created_at'].dt.date <= end_date)
    ]

    # ── 5. Main Title & Navigation Tabs ──────────────────────────────────────
    st.markdown("## 📊 AIDA Analytics Dashboard")
    st.write(f"Showing logs from `{start_date}` to `{end_date}` | OEM: `{selected_oem}` | Project: `{selected_project}` | Process: `{selected_process}`")
    
    tab_token, tab_feedback = st.tabs(["⚡ Token & Tool Analytics", "💬 Feedback & Customer Satisfaction"])

    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # TAB 1: TOKEN & TOOL ANALYTICS
    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    with tab_token:
        # KPI Metric Cards
        col1, col2, col3, col4 = st.columns(4)
        
        with col1:
            total_tokens = filtered_df['total_tokens'].sum()
            st.markdown(f"""
            <div class="kpi-card">
                <div class="kpi-value">{total_tokens:,}</div>
                <div class="kpi-label">Total Tokens Used</div>
            </div>
            """, unsafe_allow_html=True)
            
        with col2:
            input_tokens = filtered_df['input_tokens'].sum()
            st.markdown(f"""
            <div class="kpi-card">
                <div class="kpi-value">{input_tokens:,}</div>
                <div class="kpi-label">Input Prompt Tokens</div>
            </div>
            """, unsafe_allow_html=True)
            
        with col3:
            output_tokens = filtered_df['output_tokens'].sum()
            st.markdown(f"""
            <div class="kpi-card">
                <div class="kpi-value">{output_tokens:,}</div>
                <div class="kpi-label">Output Gen Tokens</div>
            </div>
            """, unsafe_allow_html=True)
            
        with col4:
            tool_invocations = filtered_df['tool_calls_count'].sum()
            st.markdown(f"""
            <div class="kpi-card">
                <div class="kpi-value">{tool_invocations:,}</div>
                <div class="kpi-label">Total Tool Calls</div>
            </div>
            """, unsafe_allow_html=True)
            
        st.markdown("---")
        
        # Visual breakdown charts
        chart_col1, chart_col2 = st.columns(2)
        
        with chart_col1:
            st.markdown("### Token Distribution Ratio")
            if total_tokens > 0:
                ratio_df = pd.DataFrame({
                    "Type": ["Input Tokens", "Output Tokens"],
                    "Count": [input_tokens, output_tokens]
                })
                fig_ratio = px.pie(
                    ratio_df,
                    names='Type',
                    values='Count',
                    color='Type',
                    color_discrete_map={'Input Tokens': '#4361ee', 'Output Tokens': '#2eca7f'},
                    hole=0.45
                )
                fig_ratio.update_layout(
                    paper_bgcolor='rgba(0,0,0,0)',
                    plot_bgcolor='rgba(0,0,0,0)',
                    font_family="Inter",
                    font_color="#888888"
                )
                st.plotly_chart(fig_ratio, use_container_width=True)
            else:
                st.info("No token records in selected context.")
                
        with chart_col2:
            st.markdown("### Token Allocation by Tool Category")
            if not filtered_df.empty:
                token_by_tool = filtered_df.groupby('tool_used')['total_tokens'].sum().reset_index()
                fig_tool_tokens = px.bar(
                    token_by_tool,
                    x='total_tokens',
                    y='tool_used',
                    orientation='h',
                    color='tool_used',
                    labels={'total_tokens': 'Total Tokens', 'tool_used': 'Tool Category'}
                )
                fig_tool_tokens.update_layout(
                    paper_bgcolor='rgba(0,0,0,0)',
                    plot_bgcolor='rgba(0,0,0,0)',
                    font_family="Inter",
                    font_color="#888888",
                    showlegend=False
                )
                st.plotly_chart(fig_tool_tokens, use_container_width=True)
            else:
                st.info("No logs found.")
                
        st.markdown("---")
        
        # Data-Heavy Tool Invocation Matrix
        st.markdown("### 🛠️ Detailed Tool Invocation Matrix (By OEM, Project, Process)")
        if not filtered_df.empty:
            matrix_df = filtered_df.groupby(['oem', 'project_key', 'process_name', 'tool_used']).agg(
                invocations=('tool_calls_count', 'sum'),
                total_tokens=('total_tokens', 'sum'),
                avg_tokens=('total_tokens', 'mean'),
                turns_handled=('id', 'count')
            ).reset_index()
            
            # Format numbers
            matrix_df['avg_tokens'] = matrix_df['avg_tokens'].round(1)
            
            st.dataframe(
                matrix_df.rename(columns={
                    "oem": "OEM",
                    "project_key": "Project Key",
                    "process_name": "Process",
                    "tool_used": "Tool/Intent Name",
                    "invocations": "Tool Executions",
                    "total_tokens": "Total Tokens Used",
                    "avg_tokens": "Avg Tokens/Turn",
                    "turns_handled": "Total Chat Turns"
                }),
                use_container_width=True
            )
        else:
            st.info("No data in selected context.")

    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # TAB 2: FEEDBACK & CUSTOMER SATISFACTION
    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    with tab_feedback:
        # Ratings calculations
        fb_df = filtered_df[filtered_df['feedback'].notna() & (filtered_df['feedback'] != '')]
        total_feedback = len(fb_df)
        likes = len(fb_df[fb_df['feedback'] == 'like'])
        dislikes = len(fb_df[fb_df['feedback'] == 'dislike'])
        csat = round((likes / total_feedback) * 100, 1) if total_feedback > 0 else 100.0
        
        col1, col2, col3, col4 = st.columns(4)
        
        with col1:
            st.markdown(f"""
            <div class="kpi-card">
                <div class="kpi-value">{csat}%</div>
                <div class="kpi-label">Satisfaction Rate (CSAT)</div>
            </div>
            """, unsafe_allow_html=True)
            
        with col2:
            st.markdown(f"""
            <div class="kpi-card">
                <div class="kpi-value">{total_feedback}</div>
                <div class="kpi-label">Total Feedbacks</div>
            </div>
            """, unsafe_allow_html=True)
            
        with col3:
            st.markdown(f"""
            <div class="kpi-card" style="border-bottom: 4px solid #2eca7f;">
                <div class="kpi-value" style="color: #2eca7f;">{likes}</div>
                <div class="kpi-label">Thumbs Up (Likes)</div>
            </div>
            """, unsafe_allow_html=True)
            
        with col4:
            st.markdown(f"""
            <div class="kpi-card" style="border-bottom: 4px solid #e05c5c;">
                <div class="kpi-value" style="color: #e05c5c;">{dislikes}</div>
                <div class="kpi-label">Thumbs Down (Dislikes)</div>
            </div>
            """, unsafe_allow_html=True)
            
        st.markdown("---")
        
        chart_col1, chart_col2 = st.columns(2)
        
        with chart_col1:
            st.markdown("### User Ratings Breakdown")
            if total_feedback > 0:
                ratings_df = pd.DataFrame({
                    "Rating": ["Likes (👍)", "Dislikes (👎)"],
                    "Count": [likes, dislikes]
                })
                fig_ratings = px.pie(
                    ratings_df,
                    names='Rating',
                    values='Count',
                    color='Rating',
                    color_discrete_map={'Likes (👍)': '#2eca7f', 'Dislikes (👎)': '#e05c5c'},
                    hole=0.45
                )
                fig_ratings.update_layout(
                    paper_bgcolor='rgba(0,0,0,0)',
                    plot_bgcolor='rgba(0,0,0,0)',
                    font_family="Inter",
                    font_color="#888888"
                )
                st.plotly_chart(fig_ratings, use_container_width=True)
            else:
                st.info("No ratings received in the selected context.")
                
        with chart_col2:
            st.markdown("### Tool-Wise Satisfaction Comparison")
            if total_feedback > 0:
                tool_csat_df = fb_df.groupby(['tool_used', 'feedback']).size().unstack(fill_value=0).reset_index()
                
                # Verify columns exist
                if 'like' not in tool_csat_df.columns:
                    tool_csat_df['like'] = 0
                if 'dislike' not in tool_csat_df.columns:
                    tool_csat_df['dislike'] = 0
                    
                fig_tool_csat = px.bar(
                    tool_csat_df,
                    x='tool_used',
                    y=['like', 'dislike'],
                    barmode='group',
                    labels={'value': 'Count', 'variable': 'Rating', 'tool_used': 'Tool Category'},
                    color_discrete_map={'like': '#2eca7f', 'dislike': '#e05c5c'}
                )
                fig_tool_csat.update_layout(
                    paper_bgcolor='rgba(0,0,0,0)',
                    plot_bgcolor='rgba(0,0,0,0)',
                    font_family="Inter",
                    font_color="#888888"
                )
                st.plotly_chart(fig_tool_csat, use_container_width=True)
            else:
                st.info("No feedback metrics received yet.")
                
        st.markdown("---")
        
        # Improvement suggestions
        st.markdown("### 💬 Improvement Opportunities (Disliked Chats with Comments)")
        disliked_df = filtered_df[filtered_df['feedback'] == 'dislike'][
            ['created_at', 'oem', 'project_key', 'process_name', 'question', 'response_given', 'comment']
        ]
        
        if not disliked_df.empty:
            st.dataframe(
                disliked_df.rename(columns={
                    "created_at": "Timestamp",
                    "oem": "OEM",
                    "project_key": "Project Key",
                    "process_name": "Process",
                    "question": "User Query",
                    "response_given": "AI Response",
                    "comment": "Correction / Improvement Suggestion"
                }),
                use_container_width=True
            )
        else:
            st.success("No improvement suggestions in the selected context!")

        st.markdown("---")
        
        # General search logs
        st.markdown("### 🔍 Telemetry Search & Raw Logs")
        search_query = st.text_input("Filter raw logs by keyword (searches query and response content)")
        
        search_df = filtered_df.copy()
        if search_query:
            search_df = search_df[
                search_df['question'].str.contains(search_query, case=False, na=False) |
                search_df['response_given'].str.contains(search_query, case=False, na=False)
            ]
            
        st.dataframe(
            search_df[['created_at', 'session_id', 'question', 'response_given', 'tool_used', 'total_tokens', 'feedback', 'comment']].rename(columns={
                "created_at": "Timestamp",
                "session_id": "Session ID",
                "question": "User Query",
                "response_given": "AI Response",
                "tool_used": "Tool Intent",
                "total_tokens": "Tokens Used",
                "feedback": "Feedback",
                "comment": "Comment"
            }),
            use_container_width=True
        )


# ── 6. Trigger Sync Analytics & Access Management ────────────────────────────
# These tabs are intentionally independent of conversation telemetry so they
# remain available even when the conversations table has no records.
from app.trigger_sync_access.service import TriggerSyncAccessService

trigger_sync_access_service = TriggerSyncAccessService()


def load_trigger_sync_audit_data():
    query = """
        SELECT
            id,
            job_id,
            triggered_by,
            source,
            oem,
            project_key,
            process_name,
            requested_defect_count,
            status,
            total,
            succeeded,
            failed,
            error_message,
            requested_at,
            completed_at
        FROM trigger_sync_audit
        ORDER BY requested_at DESC;
    """
    try:
        with get_pg_connection() as conn:
            audit_df = pd.read_sql(query, conn)
        if not audit_df.empty:
            audit_df["requested_at"] = pd.to_datetime(audit_df["requested_at"])
            audit_df["completed_at"] = pd.to_datetime(audit_df["completed_at"])
            for column in [
                "requested_defect_count", "total", "succeeded", "failed"
            ]:
                audit_df[column] = pd.to_numeric(
                    audit_df[column], errors="coerce"
                ).fillna(0)
            audit_df["execution_seconds"] = (
                audit_df["completed_at"] - audit_df["requested_at"]
            ).dt.total_seconds()
        return audit_df
    except Exception as exc:
        st.error(f"Unable to load Trigger Sync audit records: {exc}")
        return pd.DataFrame()


def render_sync_kpi(label, value):
    st.markdown(
        f"""
        <div class="kpi-card">
            <div class="kpi-value">{value:,}</div>
            <div class="kpi-label">{label}</div>
        </div>
        """,
        unsafe_allow_html=True,
    )


st.markdown("## 🔄 Trigger Sync Management")
tab_sync_analytics, tab_sync_access = st.tabs(
    ["📈 Trigger Sync Analytics", "👥 Trigger Sync Access Management"]
)

# ── TAB 3: TRIGGER SYNC ANALYTICS ─────────────────────────────────────────────
with tab_sync_analytics:
    audit_df = load_trigger_sync_audit_data()

    if audit_df.empty:
        st.info("No Trigger Sync audit records found yet.")
    else:
        st.markdown("### Execution Filters")
        filter_col1, filter_col2, filter_col3, filter_col4 = st.columns(4)

        with filter_col1:
            audit_oems = sorted(audit_df["oem"].fillna("N/A").unique().tolist())
            selected_audit_oem = st.selectbox(
                "OEM", ["All"] + audit_oems, key="sync_filter_oem"
            )
        with filter_col2:
            audit_projects = sorted(
                audit_df.loc[
                    (audit_df["oem"].fillna("N/A") == selected_audit_oem)
                    if selected_audit_oem != "All" else audit_df.index.isin(audit_df.index),
                    "project_key",
                ].dropna().unique().tolist()
            )
            selected_audit_project = st.selectbox(
                "Project", ["All"] + audit_projects, key="sync_filter_project"
            )
        with filter_col3:
            audit_process_source = audit_df.copy()
            if selected_audit_oem != "All":
                audit_process_source = audit_process_source[
                    audit_process_source["oem"].fillna("N/A") == selected_audit_oem
                ]
            if selected_audit_project != "All":
                audit_process_source = audit_process_source[
                    audit_process_source["project_key"] == selected_audit_project
                ]
            audit_processes = sorted(
                audit_process_source["process_name"].dropna().unique().tolist()
            )
            selected_audit_process = st.selectbox(
                "Process", ["All"] + audit_processes, key="sync_filter_process"
            )
        with filter_col4:
            audit_statuses = sorted(audit_df["status"].dropna().unique().tolist())
            selected_audit_status = st.selectbox(
                "Status", ["All"] + audit_statuses, key="sync_filter_status"
            )

        user_col, date_col = st.columns([1, 2])
        with user_col:
            audit_users = sorted(audit_df["triggered_by"].dropna().unique().tolist())
            selected_audit_user = st.selectbox(
                "Triggered By", ["All"] + audit_users, key="sync_filter_user"
            )
        with date_col:
            min_audit_date = audit_df["requested_at"].min().date()
            max_audit_date = audit_df["requested_at"].max().date()
            audit_date_range = st.date_input(
                "Requested Date Range",
                value=(min_audit_date, max_audit_date),
                min_value=min_audit_date,
                max_value=max_audit_date,
                key="sync_filter_dates",
            )

        filtered_audit_df = audit_df.copy()
        if selected_audit_oem != "All":
            filtered_audit_df = filtered_audit_df[
                filtered_audit_df["oem"].fillna("N/A") == selected_audit_oem
            ]
        if selected_audit_project != "All":
            filtered_audit_df = filtered_audit_df[
                filtered_audit_df["project_key"] == selected_audit_project
            ]
        if selected_audit_process != "All":
            filtered_audit_df = filtered_audit_df[
                filtered_audit_df["process_name"] == selected_audit_process
            ]
        if selected_audit_status != "All":
            filtered_audit_df = filtered_audit_df[
                filtered_audit_df["status"] == selected_audit_status
            ]
        if selected_audit_user != "All":
            filtered_audit_df = filtered_audit_df[
                filtered_audit_df["triggered_by"] == selected_audit_user
            ]
        if len(audit_date_range) == 2:
            filtered_audit_df = filtered_audit_df[
                filtered_audit_df["requested_at"].dt.date.between(
                    audit_date_range[0], audit_date_range[1]
                )
            ]

        total_jobs = len(filtered_audit_df)
        completed_jobs = int((filtered_audit_df["status"] == "COMPLETED").sum())
        failed_jobs = int((filtered_audit_df["status"] == "FAILED").sum())
        started_jobs = int((filtered_audit_df["status"] == "STARTED").sum())
        requested_defects = int(filtered_audit_df["requested_defect_count"].sum())
        processed_defects = int(filtered_audit_df["total"].sum())
        succeeded_defects = int(filtered_audit_df["succeeded"].sum())
        failed_defects = int(filtered_audit_df["failed"].sum())

        st.markdown("---")
        st.markdown("### Execution Overview")
        k1, k2, k3, k4 = st.columns(4)
        with k1:
            render_sync_kpi("Total Sync Jobs", total_jobs)
        with k2:
            render_sync_kpi("Completed Jobs", completed_jobs)
        with k3:
            render_sync_kpi("Failed Jobs", failed_jobs)
        with k4:
            render_sync_kpi("In Progress", started_jobs)

        st.markdown("### Defect Processing")
        d1, d2, d3, d4 = st.columns(4)
        with d1:
            render_sync_kpi("Defects Requested", requested_defects)
        with d2:
            render_sync_kpi("Defects Processed", processed_defects)
        with d3:
            render_sync_kpi("Successfully Synced", succeeded_defects)
        with d4:
            render_sync_kpi("Failed Defects", failed_defects)

        st.markdown("---")
        chart_left, chart_right = st.columns(2)
        with chart_left:
            st.markdown("### Defect Execution Results")
            if succeeded_defects + failed_defects > 0:
                defect_result_df = pd.DataFrame(
                    {
                        "Result": ["Succeeded", "Failed"],
                        "Defects": [succeeded_defects, failed_defects],
                    }
                )
                fig_defects = px.pie(
                    defect_result_df,
                    names="Result",
                    values="Defects",
                    color="Result",
                    color_discrete_map={
                        "Succeeded": "#2eca7f",
                        "Failed": "#e05c5c",
                    },
                    hole=0.45,
                )
                fig_defects.update_layout(
                    paper_bgcolor="rgba(0,0,0,0)",
                    plot_bgcolor="rgba(0,0,0,0)",
                    font_family="Inter",
                    font_color="#888888",
                )
                st.plotly_chart(fig_defects, use_container_width=True)
            else:
                st.info("No defect execution results in the selected filters.")

        with chart_right:
            st.markdown("### Sync Jobs by User")
            if not filtered_audit_df.empty:
                user_activity = (
                    filtered_audit_df.groupby("triggered_by")
                    .size()
                    .reset_index(name="sync_jobs")
                    .sort_values("sync_jobs", ascending=True)
                )
                fig_users = px.bar(
                    user_activity,
                    x="sync_jobs",
                    y="triggered_by",
                    orientation="h",
                    labels={
                        "sync_jobs": "Sync Jobs",
                        "triggered_by": "User",
                    },
                )
                fig_users.update_layout(
                    paper_bgcolor="rgba(0,0,0,0)",
                    plot_bgcolor="rgba(0,0,0,0)",
                    font_family="Inter",
                    font_color="#888888",
                )
                st.plotly_chart(fig_users, use_container_width=True)

        st.markdown("### Project / Process Breakdown")
        if not filtered_audit_df.empty:
            project_activity = (
                filtered_audit_df.groupby(
                    ["oem", "project_key", "process_name"], dropna=False
                )
                .agg(
                    sync_jobs=("job_id", "count"),
                    requested_defects=("requested_defect_count", "sum"),
                    processed=("total", "sum"),
                    succeeded=("succeeded", "sum"),
                    failed=("failed", "sum"),
                )
                .reset_index()
            )
            st.dataframe(project_activity, use_container_width=True)

        st.markdown("---")
        st.markdown("### Trigger Sync Audit History")
        search_audit = st.text_input(
            "Search audit history (job ID, user, project, process, error)",
            key="sync_audit_search",
        )
        audit_display_df = filtered_audit_df.copy()
        if search_audit:
            searchable = audit_display_df[
                [
                    "job_id", "triggered_by", "project_key",
                    "process_name", "error_message",
                ]
            ].fillna("").astype(str)
            matches = searchable.apply(
                lambda col: col.str.contains(search_audit, case=False, na=False)
            ).any(axis=1)
            audit_display_df = audit_display_df[matches]

        display_columns = [
            "job_id", "triggered_by", "source", "oem", "project_key",
            "process_name", "requested_defect_count", "status", "total",
            "succeeded", "failed", "requested_at", "completed_at",
            "execution_seconds", "error_message",
        ]
        st.dataframe(
            audit_display_df[display_columns].rename(
                columns={
                    "job_id": "Job ID",
                    "triggered_by": "Triggered By",
                    "source": "Source",
                    "oem": "OEM",
                    "project_key": "Project",
                    "process_name": "Process",
                    "requested_defect_count": "Requested Defects",
                    "status": "Job Status",
                    "total": "Processed",
                    "succeeded": "Succeeded",
                    "failed": "Failed",
                    "requested_at": "Requested At",
                    "completed_at": "Completed At",
                    "execution_seconds": "Duration (Seconds)",
                    "error_message": "Error Message",
                }
            ),
            use_container_width=True,
            hide_index=True,
        )

# ── TAB 4: TRIGGER SYNC ACCESS MANAGEMENT ─────────────────────────────────────
with tab_sync_access:
    st.markdown("### Manage Trigger Sync Permissions")
    st.caption(
        "ADMIN has global access. USER access is assigned to an individual project."
    )

    try:
        available_projects = trigger_sync_access_service.get_available_projects()
        project_labels = {
            item["project_key"]: (
                f'{item["oem_name"]} | {item["project_name"]} '
                f'({item["project_key"]})'
            )
            for item in available_projects
        }
        project_keys = list(project_labels.keys())

        with st.form("add_trigger_sync_access_form", clear_on_submit=True):
            st.markdown("#### Add User Access")
            add_col1, add_col2, add_col3 = st.columns([2, 1, 2])
            with add_col1:
                new_email = st.text_input("User Email", placeholder="user@company.com")
            with add_col2:
                new_role = st.selectbox("Role", ["USER", "ADMIN"])
            with add_col3:
                if project_keys:
                    new_project = st.selectbox(
                        "Project (ignored for ADMIN)", project_keys,
                        format_func=lambda key: project_labels[key],
                    )
                else:
                    new_project = None
                    st.warning("No projects found in the projects configuration table.")

            add_access_submitted = st.form_submit_button(
                "Add / Reactivate Access", type="primary", disabled=(new_role == "USER" and not project_keys)
            )

        if add_access_submitted:
            try:
                trigger_sync_access_service.add_user_access(
                    new_email, new_role, new_project
                )
                st.success(
                    f"{new_role} access saved for {new_email.strip().lower()}."
                )
                st.rerun()
            except Exception as exc:
                st.error(f"Unable to save access: {exc}")

        st.markdown("---")
        st.markdown("#### Existing Access Records")
        access_records = trigger_sync_access_service.get_all_access_users()

        if not access_records:
            st.info("No Trigger Sync access records found.")
        else:
            access_df = pd.DataFrame(access_records)
            access_display = access_df.copy()
            access_display["project_scope"] = access_display["project_key"].apply(
                lambda key: "All Projects" if key == "*" else project_labels.get(key, key)
            )
            st.dataframe(
                access_display[
                    ["id", "email", "role", "project_scope", "active", "created_at", "updated_at"]
                ].rename(
                    columns={
                        "id": "ID",
                        "email": "Email",
                        "role": "Role",
                        "project_scope": "Project Scope",
                        "active": "Active",
                        "created_at": "Created At",
                        "updated_at": "Updated At",
                    }
                ),
                use_container_width=True,
                hide_index=True,
            )

            st.markdown("#### Update Access")
            record_options = {
                int(row["id"]): (
                    f'{row["email"]} | {row["role"]} | '
                    f'{"All Projects" if row["project_key"] == "*" else row["project_key"]} '
                    f'| {"Active" if row["active"] else "Inactive"}'
                )
                for row in access_records
            }
            selected_access_id = st.selectbox(
                "Select access record",
                list(record_options.keys()),
                format_func=lambda access_id: record_options[access_id],
                key="selected_trigger_sync_access_id",
            )
            selected_record = next(
                row for row in access_records
                if int(row["id"]) == selected_access_id
            )

            with st.form("update_trigger_sync_access_form"):
                edit_col1, edit_col2, edit_col3 = st.columns([1, 2, 2])
                with edit_col1:
                    updated_role = st.selectbox(
                        "Role",
                        ["USER", "ADMIN"],
                        index=0 if selected_record["role"] == "USER" else 1,
                    )
                with edit_col2:
                    user_project_options = project_keys or [selected_record["project_key"]]
                    current_project = (
                        selected_record["project_key"]
                        if selected_record["project_key"] in user_project_options
                        else user_project_options[0]
                    )
                    updated_project = st.selectbox(
                        "Project (ignored for ADMIN)",
                        user_project_options,
                        index=user_project_options.index(current_project),
                        format_func=lambda key: project_labels.get(key, key),
                    )
                with edit_col3:
                    updated_active = st.checkbox(
                        "Access Active",
                        value=bool(selected_record["active"]),
                    )

                update_submitted = st.form_submit_button("Save Changes", type="primary")

            if update_submitted:
                try:
                    trigger_sync_access_service.update_user_access(
                        selected_access_id, updated_role, updated_project
                    )
                    trigger_sync_access_service.update_access_status(
                        selected_access_id, updated_active
                    )
                    st.success("Access record updated successfully.")
                    st.rerun()
                except Exception as exc:
                    st.error(f"Unable to update access: {exc}")

    except Exception as exc:
        st.error(f"Unable to load Trigger Sync access management: {exc}")



########################################################################################################################
FILE: app/monitoring/schema.py
FULL_PATH: /opt/AIDA/aida_react/backend/app/monitoring/schema.py
########################################################################################################################

import argparse
from app.db.connection import get_pg_connection
from app.utils.logger import setup_logger

logger = setup_logger()

CREATE_TABLE_SQL = """
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS conversations (
    id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    session_id UUID NOT NULL,
    question TEXT NOT NULL,
    response_given TEXT NOT NULL,
    feedback VARCHAR(10), -- 'like' or 'dislike'
    comment TEXT,         -- statement of what should be improved
    input_tokens INT DEFAULT 0,
    output_tokens INT DEFAULT 0,
    total_tokens INT DEFAULT 0,
    tool_calls_count INT DEFAULT 0,
    tool_used VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT 'general', -- intent/category of request
    oem VARCHAR(100),
    project_key VARCHAR(100),
    process_name VARCHAR(255),
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_conversations_session ON conversations(session_id);
"""

DROP_TABLE_SQL = "DROP TABLE IF EXISTS conversations CASCADE;"

def init_db():
    logger.info("Initializing monitoring database...")
    try:
        with get_pg_connection() as conn:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute(CREATE_TABLE_SQL)
                conn.commit()
        logger.info("Monitoring database initialized successfully.")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error("Failed to initialize monitoring database: %s", e)
        raise

def reset_db():
    logger.warning("Resetting monitoring database (dropping tables)...")
    try:
        with get_pg_connection() as conn:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute(DROP_TABLE_SQL)
                cur.execute(CREATE_TABLE_SQL)
                conn.commit()
        logger.info("Monitoring database reset successfully.")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error("Failed to reset monitoring database: %s", e)
        raise

if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Manage monitoring database schema.")
    parser.add_argument("--reset", action="store_true", help="Drop and recreate the monitoring tables.")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    if args.reset:
        reset_db()
    else:
        init_db()



########################################################################################################################
FILE: app/monitoring/service.py
FULL_PATH: /opt/AIDA/aida_react/backend/app/monitoring/service.py
########################################################################################################################

from app.db.connection import get_pg_connection
from app.utils.logger import setup_logger
from typing import Optional

logger = setup_logger()

def log_conversation(session_id: str, question: str, response_given: str, input_tokens: int, output_tokens: int, tool_used: str, tool_calls_count: int, oem: str, project_key: str, process_name: str):
    logger.info("Logging conversation for session %s (tool: %s)", session_id, tool_used)
    total_tokens = (input_tokens or 0) + (output_tokens or 0)
    try:
        with get_pg_connection() as conn:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute("""
                    INSERT INTO conversations
                    (session_id, question, response_given, input_tokens, output_tokens, total_tokens, tool_calls_count, tool_used, oem, project_key, process_name)
                    VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s);
                """, (
                    session_id,
                    question,
                    response_given,
                    input_tokens or 0,
                    output_tokens or 0,
                    total_tokens,
                    tool_calls_count or 0,
                    tool_used or "general",
                    oem,
                    project_key,
                    process_name
                ))
                conn.commit()
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error("Failed to log conversation for session %s: %s", session_id, e)

def update_conversation_feedback(session_id: str, feedback: Optional[str]=None, comment: Optional[str]=None):
    logger.info("Updating feedback for session %s: %s", session_id, feedback)
    try:
        with get_pg_connection() as conn:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                # Find the most recent conversation exchange for this session
                cur.execute("""
                    UPDATE conversations
                    SET feedback = %s, comment = %s
                    WHERE id = (
                        SELECT id FROM conversations
                        WHERE session_id = %s
                        ORDER BY created_at DESC
                        LIMIT 1
                    );
                """, (feedback, comment, session_id))
                conn.commit()
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error("Failed to update feedback for session %s: %s", session_id, e)



########################################################################################################################
FILE: app/monitoring/trigger_sync_audit.py
FULL_PATH: /opt/AIDA/aida_react/backend/app/monitoring/trigger_sync_audit.py
########################################################################################################################


from datetime import datetime, timezone

from app.db.connection import get_pg_connection
from app.utils.logger import setup_logger

logger = setup_logger()


def get_defect_count(defect_ids) -> int:
    """
    Supports comma-separated strings and lists.
    """

    if not defect_ids:
        return 0

    if isinstance(defect_ids, list):
        return len([
            item for item in defect_ids
            if item is not None and str(item).strip()
        ])

    if isinstance(defect_ids, str):
        return len([
            item for item in defect_ids.split(",")
            if item.strip()
        ])

    return 0


class TriggerSyncAuditService:

    def create_audit(
        self,
        job_id: str,
        triggered_by: str,
        source: str,
        oem: str,
        project_key: str,
        process_name: str,
        defect_ids=None,
    ):
        query = """
            INSERT INTO trigger_sync_audit (
                job_id,
                triggered_by,
                source,
                oem,
                project_key,
                process_name,
                requested_defect_count,
                status
            )
            VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, 'STARTED')
        """

        with get_pg_connection() as conn:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute(
                    query,
                    (
                        job_id,
                        triggered_by,
                        source,
                        oem,
                        project_key,
                        process_name,
                        get_defect_count(defect_ids),
                    ),
                )
            conn.commit()

        logger.info(
            "Trigger Sync audit created: job=%s user=%s source=%s",
            job_id,
            triggered_by,
            source,
        )

    def mark_completed(
        self,
        job_id: str,
        total: int,
        succeeded: int,
        failed: int,
    ):
        query = """
            UPDATE trigger_sync_audit
            SET
                status = 'COMPLETED',
                total = %s,
                succeeded = %s,
                failed = %s,
                completed_at = %s
            WHERE job_id = %s
        """

        with get_pg_connection() as conn:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute(
                    query,
                    (
                        total,
                        succeeded,
                        failed,
                        datetime.now(timezone.utc).replace(tzinfo=None),
                        job_id,
                    ),
                )
            conn.commit()

    def mark_failed(
        self,
        job_id: str,
        error_message: str,
    ):
        query = """
            UPDATE trigger_sync_audit
            SET
                status = 'FAILED',
                error_message = %s,
                completed_at = %s
            WHERE job_id = %s
        """

        with get_pg_connection() as conn:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute(
                    query,
                    (
                        str(error_message)[:2000],
                        datetime.now(timezone.utc).replace(tzinfo=None),
                        job_id,
                    ),
                )
            conn.commit()

from app.db.connection import get_pg_connection
from app.utils.logger import setup_logger

logger = setup_logger()

CREATE_TRIGGER_SYNC_AUDIT_TABLE = """
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS trigger_sync_audit (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,

    job_id VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL UNIQUE,

    triggered_by VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    source VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,

    oem VARCHAR(100),
    project_key VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    process_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,

    requested_defect_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

    status VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'STARTED',

    total INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    succeeded INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    failed INTEGER DEFAULT 0,

    error_message TEXT,

    requested_at TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    completed_at TIMESTAMP
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_trigger_sync_audit_requested_at
ON trigger_sync_audit(requested_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_trigger_sync_audit_user
ON trigger_sync_audit(triggered_by);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_trigger_sync_audit_project
ON trigger_sync_audit(project_key);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_trigger_sync_audit_status
ON trigger_sync_audit(status);
"""


def init_trigger_sync_audit_db():
    try:
        with get_pg_connection() as conn:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute(CREATE_TRIGGER_SYNC_AUDIT_TABLE)
            conn.commit()

        logger.info("Trigger Sync audit table initialized.")

    except Exception:
        logger.exception("Failed to initialize Trigger Sync audit table.")
        raise

from pydantic import BaseModel
