    display_name: str

    email: str

    jira_account_id: str

    connected: bool



########################################################################################################################
FILE: app/profile/service.py
FULL_PATH: /opt/AIDA/aida_react/backend/app/profile/service.py
########################################################################################################################

from app.userSession.service import UserSessionService
from app.jiraOAuth.service import JiraOAuthService
from app.profile.schema import UserProfile


class ProfileService:

    def __init__(self):

        self.session_service = UserSessionService()

        self.jira_service = JiraOAuthService()

    async def get_profile(
        self,
        session_id: str,
    ) -> UserProfile:

        session = self.session_service.validate_session(
            session_id
        )

        if session is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Invalid session."
            )

        profile = await self.jira_service.get_profile(
            session["email"]
        )

        return UserProfile(**profile)



########################################################################################################################
FILE: app/quick_actions/router.py
FULL_PATH: /opt/AIDA/aida_react/backend/app/quick_actions/router.py
########################################################################################################################

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException
from pydantic import BaseModel

from app.auth.dependencies import get_authorization_context
from app.auth.schema import AuthorizationContext
from app.session.session_service import SessionService
from app.services.config_service import ConfigService
from app.exchange_history.service import ExchangeHistoryService
from app.conversation.service import ConversationService
from app.tools.unified_tools import _get_dashboard_process_config
import json

router = APIRouter(
    prefix="/api/quick-actions",
    tags=["Quick Actions"],
)

session_service = SessionService()
config_service = ConfigService()
exchange_history_service = ExchangeHistoryService()
conversation_service = ConversationService()


class QuickActionRequest(BaseModel):
    conversation_id: str
    chat_conversation_id: str | None = None
    timezone: str | None = None


def get_conversation_context(
    chat_conversation_id: str | None,
    authorization: AuthorizationContext,
):
    if not chat_conversation_id:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail="Chat conversation is required.",
        )

    conversation = conversation_service.get_owned_conversation(
        conversation_id=chat_conversation_id,
        user_email=authorization.email,
    )

    if conversation is None:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=404,
            detail="Chat conversation not found.",
        )

    oem = conversation.get("oem")
    project_key = conversation.get("project_key")
    process_name = conversation.get("process_name")

    if not project_key or not process_name:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail="Project and process must be selected before using this quick action.",
        )

    authorized_project = next(
        (
            project
            for project in authorization.projects
            if project.project_key == project_key
        ),
        None,
    )

    if authorized_project is None:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=403,
            detail="Project is not authorized.",
        )

    return oem, project_key, process_name


def validate_chat_conversation(
    chat_conversation_id: str | None,
    authorization: AuthorizationContext,
):
    if not chat_conversation_id:
        return

    conversation = conversation_service.get_owned_conversation(
        conversation_id=chat_conversation_id,
        user_email=authorization.email,
    )

    if conversation is None:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=404,
            detail="Chat conversation not found.",
        )


def store_quick_action_message(
    chat_conversation_id: str | None,
    authorization: AuthorizationContext,
    role: str,
    content: str,
    message_type: str = "chat",
):
    if not chat_conversation_id:
        return

    try:
        conversation_service.add_message(
            conversation_id=chat_conversation_id,
            user_email=authorization.email,
            role=role,
            content=content,
            message_type=message_type,
        )

    except Exception:
        # Quick action should still work even if chat persistence fails.
        import logging

        logging.exception(
            "Failed to persist quick action message."
        )


@router.post("/schedule")
async def quick_schedule(
    request: QuickActionRequest,
    authorization: AuthorizationContext = Depends(
        get_authorization_context
    ),
):
    # Validate runtime conversation
    _, project_key, process_name = get_conversation_context(
        request.chat_conversation_id,
        authorization,
    )

    # Validate persistent chat conversation
    validate_chat_conversation(
        request.chat_conversation_id,
        authorization,
    )

    # Store user action
    store_quick_action_message(
        chat_conversation_id=request.chat_conversation_id,
        authorization=authorization,
        role="user",
        content="Show exchange schedule",
    )

    result = config_service.get_schedule(
        project_key=project_key,
        process_name=process_name,
        timezone=request.timezone,
    )

    response = {
        "status": "success",
        "action": "schedule",
        "data": result,
    }

    # Store assistant response
    store_quick_action_message(
        chat_conversation_id=request.chat_conversation_id,
        authorization=authorization,
        role="assistant",
        content=json.dumps(response),
        message_type="quick_action_schedule",
    )

    return response


@router.post("/mapping")
async def quick_mapping(
    request: QuickActionRequest,
    authorization: AuthorizationContext = Depends(
        get_authorization_context
    ),
):
    oem, project_key, process_name = get_conversation_context(
        request.chat_conversation_id,
        authorization,
    )

    if not oem:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail="OEM must be selected before using the mapping quick action.",
        )

    
    store_quick_action_message(
        chat_conversation_id=request.chat_conversation_id,
        authorization=authorization,
        role="user",
        content="Show mapping information",
    )

    result = config_service.get_mapping_summary(
        project_key=project_key,
        process_name=process_name,
    )

    response = {
        "status": "success",
        "action": "mapping",
        "data": result,
    }

    store_quick_action_message(
        chat_conversation_id=request.chat_conversation_id,
        authorization=authorization,
        role="assistant",
        content=json.dumps(response),
        message_type="quick_action_mapping",
    )

    return response


@router.post("/exchange-history")
async def quick_exchange_history(
    request: QuickActionRequest,
    authorization: AuthorizationContext = Depends(
        get_authorization_context
    ),
):

    oem,project_key, process_name = get_conversation_context(
        request.chat_conversation_id,
        authorization,
    )
    

    validate_chat_conversation(
        request.chat_conversation_id,
        authorization,
    )

    store_quick_action_message(
        chat_conversation_id=request.chat_conversation_id,
        authorization=authorization,
        role="user",
        content="Show recent exchange history",
    )

    try:
        customer, dashboard_project, dashboard_process = (
            _get_dashboard_process_config(oem, project_key, process_name)
        )

        print(f"Resolved dashboard process configuration:")
        print(f"  OEM: {oem}")
        print(f"  Project Key: {project_key}")
        print(f"  Process Name: {process_name}")
        print(f"  Customer: {customer}")
        print(f"  Dashboard Project: {dashboard_project}")
        print(f"  Dashboard Process: {dashboard_process}")
        print("--------------------------------")

        result = exchange_history_service.get_failure_summary_direct(
            project_key=project_key,
            hours=24,
            customer=customer,
            project=dashboard_project,
            process_name=dashboard_process,
        )
    
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail=str(exc),
        ) from exc
    
    except Exception as exc:
        import logging
        logging.exception("Failed to retrieve recent exchange history")
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=500,
            detail="An error occurred while fetching exchange history.",
        ) from exc
    


    response = {
        "status": "success",
        "action": "exchange-history",
        "data": result,
    }

    store_quick_action_message(
        chat_conversation_id=request.chat_conversation_id,
        authorization=authorization,
        role="assistant",
        content=json.dumps(response),
        message_type="quick_action_exchange_history",
    )

    return response



########################################################################################################################
FILE: app/rca/error_knowledge_repository.py
FULL_PATH: /opt/AIDA/aida_react/backend/app/rca/error_knowledge_repository.py
########################################################################################################################

import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def find_by_signature(
    conn,
    error_signature: str,
    limit: int = 5
):
    """
    Retrieve active error knowledge records by error signature.

    Returns matching records as a list of dictionaries.
    """

    cur = conn.cursor()

    try:
        cur.execute("""
            SELECT
                id,
                error_message,
                error_signature,
                failure_type,
                failure_stage,
                possible_root_cause,
                resolution,
                required_tools
            FROM error_knowledge
            WHERE error_signature = %s
              AND is_active = TRUE
            ORDER BY id
            LIMIT %s
        """, (
            error_signature,
            limit
        ))

        columns = [desc[0] for desc in cur.description]

        return [
            dict(zip(columns, row))
            for row in cur.fetchall()
        ]

    finally:
        cur.close()


def find_by_error_message(
    conn,
    error_message: str,
    limit: int = 5
):
    """
    Retrieve active error knowledge records by exact
    case-insensitive error message match.
    """

    cur = conn.cursor()

    try:
        cur.execute("""
            SELECT
                id,
                error_message,
                error_signature,
                failure_type,
                failure_stage,
                possible_root_cause,
                resolution,
                required_tools
            FROM error_knowledge
            WHERE LOWER(TRIM(error_message)) =
                  LOWER(TRIM(%s))
              AND is_active = TRUE
            ORDER BY id
            LIMIT %s
        """, (
            error_message,
            limit
        ))

        columns = [desc[0] for desc in cur.description]

        return [
            dict(zip(columns, row))
            for row in cur.fetchall()
        ]

    finally:
        cur.close()



def search_similar_errors(
    conn,
    error_message: str,
    limit: int = 5
):
    """
    Retrieve active error knowledge records with
    similar error messages using PostgreSQL trigram search.

    Returns matching records ordered by similarity.
    """

    cur = conn.cursor()

    try:
        cur.execute("""
            SELECT
                id,
                error_message,
                error_signature,
                failure_type,
                failure_stage,
                possible_root_cause,
                resolution,
                required_tools,
                similarity(
                    LOWER(error_message),
                    LOWER(%s)
                ) AS similarity_score

            FROM error_knowledge

            WHERE is_active = TRUE
              AND LOWER(error_message) %% LOWER(%s)

            ORDER BY similarity_score DESC
            LIMIT %s
        """, (
            error_message,
            error_message,
            limit
        ))

        columns = [desc[0] for desc in cur.description]

        return [
            dict(zip(columns, row))
            for row in cur.fetchall()
        ]

    finally:
        cur.close()



########################################################################################################################
FILE: app/rca/error_knowledge_service.py
FULL_PATH: /opt/AIDA/aida_react/backend/app/rca/error_knowledge_service.py
########################################################################################################################


from app.rca import error_knowledge_repository as err_repo


class ErrorKnowledgeRetrievalService:

    def __init__(self, min_similarity: float = 0.30):
        self.min_similarity = min_similarity

    def _build_result(
        self,
        match_type: str,
        matches: list,
    ) -> dict:
        """
        Build a consistent retrieval response.

        A record is selected automatically only when there is
        exactly one matching record.
        """

        selected = matches[0] if len(matches) == 1 else None

        return {
            "status": "success",
            "matched": bool(matches),
            "match_type": match_type if matches else None,
            "ambiguous": len(matches) > 1,
            "selected": selected,
            "matches": matches,
        }

    def retrieve(
        self,
        conn,
        error_message: str,
        error_signature: str = None,
        limit: int = 5,
    ) -> dict:
        """
        Retrieve historical error knowledge.

        Priority:
            1. Exact error message
            2. Error signature
            3. Similar error message

        The caller owns the database connection.
        """

        if not error_message or not error_message.strip():
            return {
                "status": "invalid_input",
                "matched": False,
                "match_type": None,
                "ambiguous": False,
                "selected": None,
                "matches": [],
                "message": "Error message is required.",
            }

        # STEP 1: Exact error message
        exact_matches = err_repo.find_by_error_message(
            conn,
            error_message,
            limit=limit,
        )

        if exact_matches:
            return self._build_result(
                match_type="exact",
                matches=exact_matches,
            )

        # STEP 2: Error signature
        signature_matches = []

        if error_signature:
            signature_matches = err_repo.find_by_signature(
                conn,
                error_signature,
                limit=limit,
            )

            # A signature may represent multiple error records.
            # Do not arbitrarily select one.
            if len(signature_matches) == 1:
                return self._build_result(
                    match_type="signature",
                    matches=signature_matches,
                )

        # STEP 3: Similar error message
        similar_matches = err_repo.search_similar_errors(
            conn,
            error_message,
            limit=limit,
        )

        similar_matches = [
            match
            for match in similar_matches
            if float(match.get("similarity_score") or 0)
            >= self.min_similarity
        ]

        # If a signature matched multiple records, use similarity
        # to disambiguate within those signature records.
        if signature_matches:
            signature_ids = {
                match["id"]
                for match in signature_matches
            }

            signature_similar_matches = [
                match
                for match in similar_matches
                if match["id"] in signature_ids
            ]

            if signature_similar_matches:
                return self._build_result(
                    match_type="signature_and_similarity",
                    matches=[signature_similar_matches[0]],
                )

            return self._build_result(
                match_type="signature",
                matches=signature_matches,
            )

        if similar_matches:
            # Similarity results are ordered highest first
            # by the repository query.
            return self._build_result(
                match_type="similar",
                matches=[similar_matches[0]],
            )

        return {
            "status": "success",
            "matched": False,
            "match_type": None,
            "ambiguous": False,
            "selected": None,
            "matches": [],
        }



########################################################################################################################
FILE: app/rca/repository.py
FULL_PATH: /opt/AIDA/aida_react/backend/app/rca/repository.py
########################################################################################################################


import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def resolve_process_candidates(
    conn,
    customer: str,
    project: str,
    process_name: str
):
    """
    Resolve Oracle exchange identity to AIDA process records.

    Matching:
        Oracle CUSTOMERNAME  -> config_entries.DCustomer
        Oracle PROJECTNAME   -> config_entries.DProjectName
        Oracle PROCESSNAME   -> config_entries.DProcessName

    Returns all matches so the caller can detect ambiguity.
    """

    cur = conn.cursor()

    try:
        cur.execute("""
            WITH process_config AS (
                SELECT
                    process_id,

                    MAX(CASE
                        WHEN config_key = 'DCustomer'
                        THEN config_value
                    END) AS customer_name,

                    MAX(CASE
                        WHEN config_key = 'DProjectName'
                        THEN config_value
                    END) AS logical_project_name,

                    MAX(CASE
                        WHEN config_key = 'DProcessName'
                        THEN config_value
                    END) AS logical_process_name,

                    MAX(CASE
                        WHEN config_key = 'SourceJiraProject'
                        THEN config_value
                    END) AS source_project_key,

                    MAX(CASE
                        WHEN config_key = 'DestinationJiraProject'
                        THEN config_value
                    END) AS destination_project_key,

                    MAX(CASE
                        WHEN config_key = 'sourceConfigSet'
                        THEN config_value
                    END) AS source_configset_id,

                    MAX(CASE
                        WHEN config_key = 'destinationConfigSet'
                        THEN config_value
                    END) AS destination_configset_id

                FROM config_entries
                WHERE config_key IN (
                    'DCustomer',
                    'DProjectName',
                    'DProcessName',
                    'SourceJiraProject',
                    'DestinationJiraProject',
                    'sourceConfigSet',
                    'destinationConfigSet'
                )
                GROUP BY process_id
            )

            SELECT
                p.id AS process_id,
                p.process_name AS scheduler_process_name,
                p.java_process_name,
                p.configset_name AS process_configset_name,
                p.instance AS process_instance,

                pc.customer_name,
                pc.logical_project_name,
                pc.logical_process_name,

                pc.source_project_key,
                pc.destination_project_key,

                pc.source_configset_id,
                sac.configset_name AS source_configset_name,
                sac.alm_type AS source_alm_type,

                pc.destination_configset_id,
                dac.configset_name AS destination_configset_name,
                dac.alm_type AS destination_alm_type

            FROM processes p

            JOIN process_config pc
                ON pc.process_id = p.id

            LEFT JOIN alm_configsets sac
                ON sac.instance = p.instance
               AND sac.configset_id = pc.source_configset_id

            LEFT JOIN alm_configsets dac
                ON dac.instance = p.instance
               AND dac.configset_id = pc.destination_configset_id

            WHERE UPPER(TRIM(pc.customer_name)) = UPPER(TRIM(%s))
              AND UPPER(TRIM(pc.logical_project_name)) = UPPER(TRIM(%s))
              AND UPPER(TRIM(pc.logical_process_name)) = UPPER(TRIM(%s))

            ORDER BY p.id
        """, (
            customer,
            project,
            process_name
        ))

        columns = [desc[0] for desc in cur.description]

        return [
            dict(zip(columns, row))
            for row in cur.fetchall()
        ]

    finally:
        cur.close()



########################################################################################################################
FILE: app/rca/service.py
FULL_PATH: /opt/AIDA/aida_react/backend/app/rca/service.py
########################################################################################################################

from app.db.connection import get_pg_pool_conn as get_connection
from app.rca import repository as repo
from datetime import datetime
from app.config.settings import PROD1_CONFIG, PROD2_CONFIG, QA2_CONFIG
from app.services.jira_defect_service import JiraDefectService
from app.services.config_service import ConfigService
from app.exchange_history.service import ExchangeHistoryService


import re

class RCAService:

    
    def resolve_execution_process(self, execution_id: str):
        """
        Resolve one Oracle exchange execution to its AIDA process.

        Oracle:
            exchangeprocess
            exchangedescription

        PostgreSQL:
            processes
            config_entries
            alm_configsets
        """

        # STEP 1: Retrieve exact execution from Oracle
        history_service = ExchangeHistoryService()

        history_response = history_service.get_execution_context(
            execution_id
        )

        if history_response.get("status") != "success":
            return history_response

        execution = history_response["execution"]

        customer = execution.get("customername")
        project = execution.get("projectname")
        process_name = execution.get("processname")

        if not all((customer, project, process_name)):
            return {
                "status": "invalid_execution_identity",
                "execution_id": execution_id,
                "message": (
                    "Oracle execution is missing customer, "
                    "project, or process name."
                ),
                "execution": execution
            }

        # STEP 2: Resolve matching AIDA process in PostgreSQL
        conn = get_connection()

        try:
            candidates = repo.resolve_process_candidates(
                conn=conn,
                customer=customer,
                project=project,
                process_name=process_name
            )

        finally:
            conn.close()

        # STEP 3: Handle no matching process
        if not candidates:
            return {
                "status": "process_not_found",
                "execution_id": execution_id,
                "exchange_identity": {
                    "customer": customer,
                    "project": project,
                    "process_name": process_name
                },
                "execution": execution,
                "failed_records": history_response.get(
                    "failed_records", []
                ),
                "message": (
                    "No matching AIDA process was found "
                    "for the Oracle exchange identity."
                )
            }

        # STEP 4: Do not arbitrarily choose among multiple processes
        if len(candidates) > 1:
            return {
                "status": "ambiguous_process",
                "execution_id": execution_id,
                "exchange_identity": {
                    "customer": customer,
                    "project": project,
                    "process_name": process_name
                },
                "candidates": [
                    {
                        "process_id": item["process_id"],
                        "scheduler_process_name": (
                            item["scheduler_process_name"]
                        ),
                        "instance": item["process_instance"],
                        "process_configset_name": (
                            item["process_configset_name"]
                        ),
                        "source_project_key": (
                            item["source_project_key"]
                        ),
                        "destination_project_key": (
                            item["destination_project_key"]
                        )
                    }
                    for item in candidates
                ],
                "message": (
                    "Multiple AIDA processes match this exchange "
                    "identity. Additional disambiguation is required."
                )
            }

        # STEP 5: Exactly one matching AIDA process
        process_config = candidates[0]

        return {
            "status": "success",
            "execution_id": execution_id,
            "exchange_identity": {
                "customer": customer,
                "project": project,
                "process_name": process_name
            },
            "execution": execution,
            "process_config": process_config,
            "failed_records": history_response.get(
                "failed_records", []
            ),
            "failure_count": len(
                history_response.get("failed_records", [])
            )
        }


    



    
    def get_exchange_context(self, history_response, issue_key):

        if history_response.get("status") != "success":
            return None

        defect_history = history_response.get(
            "results", {}
        ).get(issue_key)

        if not defect_history:
            return None

        failed_attempts = []

        for direction_history in defect_history.get("history", []):

            for record in direction_history.get("raw_data", []):

                if str(record.get("status", "")).lower() != "failed":
                    continue

                # Original Oracle execution ID must be preserved
                execution_id = record.get("execution_id")

                # Fallback only if processid still contains the original ID
                if not execution_id:
                    process_id = record.get("processid")
                    if process_id and "_" in str(process_id):
                        execution_id = str(process_id)

                if not execution_id:
                    continue

                # Extract full timestamp from execution ID: YYMMDDHHMMSS
                try:
                    execution_timestamp = datetime.strptime(
                        execution_id[:12], "%y%m%d%H%M%S"
                    )
                except (ValueError, TypeError):
                    continue

                destination_id = record.get("destinationwiid")

                failed_attempts.append({
                    "execution_id": execution_id,
                    "direction": direction_history.get("direction"),
                    "failure_date": execution_timestamp,
                    "source_issue_key": record.get("sourcewiid"),
                    "destination_issue_key": (
                        None
                        if destination_id in (None, "", "null")
                        else destination_id
                    ),
                    "failure_message": record.get("description")
                })

        if not failed_attempts:
            return None

        # Select latest failed execution across both directions
        failed_attempts.sort(
            key=lambda x: x["failure_date"],
            reverse=True
        )

        latest_failure = failed_attempts[0]

        return {
            "issue_key": issue_key,
            "created": defect_history.get("Created"),

            "selected_failure": {
                "execution_id": latest_failure["execution_id"],
                "direction": latest_failure["direction"],
                "failure_date": latest_failure["failure_date"].strftime("%d-%m-%Y"),
                "source_issue_key": latest_failure["source_issue_key"],
                "destination_issue_key": latest_failure["destination_issue_key"],
                "failure_message": latest_failure["failure_message"]
            },

            "previous_failures": [
                {
                    "execution_id": attempt["execution_id"],
                    "direction": attempt["direction"],
                    "failure_date": attempt["failure_date"].strftime("%d-%m-%Y"),
                    "failure_message": attempt["failure_message"]
                }
                for attempt in failed_attempts[1:]
            ]
        }



    
    
    def prepare_issue_context(self, issue_data, relevant_fields=None):

        fields = issue_data.get("fields", {})

        if relevant_fields:
            fields = {
                name: value
                for name, value in fields.items()
                if name in relevant_fields
            }

        return {
            "jira_id": issue_data.get("key"),
            "fields": [
                {
                    "field_name": field_name,
                    "value": field_data.get("value", [])
                }
                for field_name, field_data in fields.items()
            ]
        }

    
    
    
    def identify_exchange_alm_sides(self, failure, process_config):

        source_issue_key = failure.get("source_issue_key")
        destination_issue_key = failure.get("destination_issue_key")

        def matches_project(issue_key, project_key):
            if not issue_key or not project_key:
                return False

            issue_project = issue_key.split("-")[0].upper()
            return issue_project == project_key.upper()

        source_matches_config_source = matches_project(
            source_issue_key,
            process_config.get("source_project_key")
        )

        source_matches_config_destination = matches_project(
            source_issue_key,
            process_config.get("destination_project_key")
        )

        if source_matches_config_source:
            orientation = "normal"

        elif source_matches_config_destination:
            orientation = "reverse"

        else:
            return {
                "status": "configuration_mismatch",
                "message": "Source issue does not match either configured project."
            }

        if orientation == "normal":

            source_config = {
                "is_jira": process_config["source_alm_type"].upper() == "JIRA",
                "issue_key": source_issue_key,
                "config_set_name": process_config["source_configset_name"],
                "project_key": process_config["source_project_key"],
                "instance": process_config["process_instance"],
            }

            destination_config = {
                "is_jira": process_config["destination_alm_type"].upper() == "JIRA",
                "issue_key": destination_issue_key,
                "config_set_name": process_config["destination_configset_name"],
                "project_key": process_config["destination_project_key"],
                "instance": process_config["process_instance"],
            }

        else:

            source_config = {
                "is_jira": process_config["destination_alm_type"].upper() == "JIRA",
                "issue_key": source_issue_key,
                "config_set_name": process_config["destination_configset_name"],
                "project_key": process_config["destination_project_key"],
                "instance": process_config["process_instance"],
            }

            destination_config = {
                "is_jira": process_config["source_alm_type"].upper() == "JIRA",
                "issue_key": destination_issue_key,
                "config_set_name": process_config["source_configset_name"],
                "project_key": process_config["source_project_key"],
                "instance": process_config["process_instance"],
            }

        return {
            "status": "success",
            "orientation": orientation,
            "source": source_config,
            "destination": destination_config
        }

    

    def get_alm_credentials(self, instance: str):

        instance_configs = {
            "PROD1": PROD1_CONFIG,
            "PROD2": PROD2_CONFIG,
            "QA2": QA2_CONFIG
        }

        config = instance_configs.get(instance)

        if not config:
            raise ValueError(f"Unsupported ALM instance: {instance}")

        username = config.get("username")
        password = config.get("password")
        base_url = config.get("base_url")

        if not username or not password:
            raise ValueError(f"Credentials not configured for {instance}")

        return username, password, base_url

    

    def get_jira_issue(self, alm_side):

        if not alm_side.get("is_jira"):
            raise ValueError("The selected ALM is not Jira")

        issue_key = alm_side.get("issue_key")
        config_set_name = alm_side.get("config_set_name")

        if not issue_key or not config_set_name:
            raise ValueError("Jira issue key or config set name is missing")

        username, password, base_url = self.get_alm_credentials(
            alm_side.get("instance")
        )

        jira_service = JiraDefectService(
            username=username,
            password=password,
            base_url=base_url
        )

        return jira_service.normalize_issue(
            jira_service.get_issue(config_set_name=config_set_name,issue_key=issue_key)
            )


    def get_relevant_jira_fields(
        self,
        process_id: int,
        orientation: str
    ):
        config_service = ConfigService()

        mapping_response = config_service.get_mapping_summary_by_process_id(
            process_id=process_id
        )

        if mapping_response.get("status") != "success":
            return []

        jira_fields = set()

        for scenario_mappings in mapping_response.get("mappings", {}).values():

            for mapping in scenario_mappings:

                if orientation == "normal":
                    jira_fields.add(mapping["source_field"])

                elif orientation == "reverse":
                    jira_fields.add(mapping["destination_field"])

        return sorted(jira_fields)


    



    def filter_jira_fields_by_failure(
        self,
        failure_message: str,
        mapped_fields: list
    ):
        if not failure_message or not mapped_fields:
            return mapped_fields

        def normalize(value):
            value = value.lower()
            value = re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", " ", value)
            return " ".join(value.split())

        normalized_message = normalize(failure_message)

        matched_fields = []

        for field in mapped_fields:
            normalized_field = normalize(field)

            if normalized_field and normalized_field in normalized_message:
                matched_fields.append(field)

        # If no field is identified, retain all mapped fields.
        # Avoid losing potentially relevant evidence.
        return matched_fields if matched_fields else mapped_fields

    

    def get_mapping_evidence(
        self,
        jira_context: dict,
        process_id: int,
        orientation: str
    ):
        if orientation not in ("normal", "reverse"):
            raise ValueError(f"Invalid exchange orientation: {orientation}")

        config_service = ConfigService()

        mapping_response = config_service.get_mapping_summary_by_process_id(
            process_id=process_id
        )
        

        if mapping_response.get("status") != "success":
            return {
                "status": "not_found",
                "process_id": process_id,
                "orientation": orientation,
                "fields": []
            }

        mapping_data = mapping_response.get("mappings", {})

        jira_fields = jira_context.get("fields", [])

        mapping_evidence = []

        for jira_field in jira_fields:

            field_name = jira_field.get("field_name")

            if not field_name:
                continue

            field_evidence = {
                "jira_field_name": field_name,
                "jira_value": jira_field.get("value"),
                "orientation": orientation,
                "mapping_found": False,
                "mappings": []
            }

            for scenario_name, scenario_mappings in mapping_data.items():

                for mapping in scenario_mappings:

                    if orientation == "normal":
                        jira_side_field = mapping.get("source_field")
                        other_side_field = mapping.get("destination_field")
                    else:
                        jira_side_field = mapping.get("destination_field")
                        other_side_field = mapping.get("source_field")

                    if (
                        not jira_side_field
                        or jira_side_field.strip().lower() != field_name.strip().lower()
                    ):
                        continue

                    field_evidence["mapping_found"] = True

                    field_evidence["mappings"].append({
                        "scenario_name": scenario_name,
                        "source_field": mapping.get("source_field"),
                        "destination_field": mapping.get("destination_field"),
                        "other_alm_field": other_side_field,
                        "value_mappings": mapping.get("value_mappings", [])
                    })

            mapping_evidence.append(field_evidence)

        return {
            "status": "success",
            "process_id": process_id,
            "orientation": orientation,
            "fields": mapping_evidence
        }


    
    def test_rca_flow(self, history_response, issue_key):
        """
        Diagnostic end-to-end RCA evidence collection.

        Parameters:
            history_response:
                Existing exchange history response for the issue.

            issue_key:
                Original Jira issue key used to retrieve exchange history.
        """

        result = {
            "status": "failed",
            "issue_key": issue_key,
            "steps": {}
        }

        # --------------------------------------------------
        # STEP 1: Select the latest failed execution
        # --------------------------------------------------
        exchange_context = self.get_exchange_context(
            history_response,
            issue_key
        )

        if not exchange_context:
            result["failed_at"] = "exchange_context"
            result["message"] = "No failed execution found"

            return result

        failure = exchange_context["selected_failure"]
        execution_id = failure["execution_id"]

        result["steps"]["selected_execution"] = {
            "execution_id": execution_id,
            "failure": failure
        }

        # --------------------------------------------------
        # STEP 2: Resolve Oracle identity to AIDA process
        # --------------------------------------------------
        process_resolution = self.resolve_execution_process(
            execution_id
        )

        if process_resolution.get("status") != "success":
            result["failed_at"] = "process_resolution"

            result["steps"]["process_resolution"] = (
                process_resolution
            )

            return result

        process_config = process_resolution["process_config"]
        process_id = process_config["process_id"]

        result["steps"]["oracle_identity"] = (
            process_resolution["exchange_identity"]
        )

        result["steps"]["process_resolution"] = {
            "process_id": process_id,
            "process_config": process_config
        }

        # --------------------------------------------------
        # STEP 3: Identify exchange orientation and ALM sides
        # --------------------------------------------------
        alm_sides = self.identify_exchange_alm_sides(
            failure,
            process_config
        )

        if alm_sides.get("status") != "success":
            result["failed_at"] = "alm_orientation"
            result["steps"]["alm_sides"] = alm_sides

            return result

        exchange_orientation = alm_sides["orientation"]

        result["steps"]["exchange_orientation"] = (
            exchange_orientation
        )

        result["steps"]["alm_sides"] = alm_sides

        # --------------------------------------------------
        # STEP 4: Fetch Jira issues from applicable ALM sides
        # --------------------------------------------------
        jira_results = {}

        for side_name in ("source", "destination"):

            alm_side = alm_sides[side_name]

            if not alm_side.get("is_jira"):
                continue

            jira_key = alm_side.get("issue_key")

            if not jira_key:
                continue

            try:
                jira_issue = self.get_jira_issue(alm_side)

                # Determine which configured mapping side
                # corresponds to this Jira project.
                jira_project = alm_side.get("project_key")

                if (
                    jira_project
                    == process_config.get("source_project_key")
                ):
                    jira_mapping_orientation = "normal"

                elif (
                    jira_project
                    == process_config.get("destination_project_key")
                ):
                    jira_mapping_orientation = "reverse"

                else:
                    result["failed_at"] = "jira_project_mapping"

                    result["message"] = (
                        f"Jira project {jira_project} does not "
                        "match the resolved process configuration"
                    )

                    return result

                # --------------------------------------------------
                # STEP 5: Select relevant mapped Jira fields
                # --------------------------------------------------
                mapped_fields = self.get_relevant_jira_fields(
                    process_id,
                    jira_mapping_orientation
                )

                # --------------------------------------------------
                # STEP 6: Filter fields using failure message
                # --------------------------------------------------
                filtered_fields = (
                    self.filter_jira_fields_by_failure(
                        failure.get("failure_message"),
                        mapped_fields
                    )
                )

                jira_context = self.prepare_issue_context(
                    jira_issue,
                    filtered_fields
                )

                # --------------------------------------------------
                # STEP 7: Collect mapping evidence
                # --------------------------------------------------
                mapping_evidence = self.get_mapping_evidence(
                    jira_context,
                    process_id,
                    jira_mapping_orientation
                )

                jira_results[jira_key] = {
                    "side": side_name,
                    "jira_mapping_orientation": (
                        jira_mapping_orientation
                    ),
                    "mapped_fields": mapped_fields,
                    "filtered_fields": filtered_fields,
                    "jira_context": jira_context,
                    "mapping_evidence": mapping_evidence
                }

            except Exception as exc:
                result["failed_at"] = f"jira_{side_name}"
                result["message"] = str(exc)

                result["steps"]["jira_results"] = jira_results

                return result

        # --------------------------------------------------
        # STEP 8: Build complete evidence package
        # --------------------------------------------------
        result["steps"]["jira_issue_keys_fetched"] = (
            list(jira_results.keys())
        )

        result["steps"]["jira_results"] = jira_results

        result["status"] = "success"

        return result

from typing import List, Dict, Optional


# ---------------------------------------------------
# OEM Queries
# ---------------------------------------------------

def get_oems(conn) -> List[Dict]:
    cur = conn.cursor()
    cur.execute("""
        SELECT id, name
        FROM oems
        ORDER BY name;
    """)
    rows = cur.fetchall()
    cur.close()

    return [{"id": r[0], "name": r[1]} for r in rows]


# ---------------------------------------------------
# Project Queries
# ---------------------------------------------------


def get_projects_by_oem(conn, oem_name: str) -> List[Dict]:
    cur = conn.cursor()
    cur.execute("""
        SELECT p.project_key, p.project_name
        FROM projects p
        JOIN oems o ON p.oem_id = o.id
        WHERE o.name = %s
        ORDER BY p.project_key;
    """, (oem_name.upper(),))
    rows = cur.fetchall()
    cur.close()

    return [{"project_key": r[0], "project_name": r[1]} for r in rows]


def find_project_by_key(conn, project_key: str) -> Optional[Dict]:
    cur = conn.cursor()
    cur.execute("""
        SELECT project_key, project_name
        FROM projects
        WHERE project_key = %s;
    """, (project_key,))
    row = cur.fetchone()
    cur.close()

    if not row:
        return None

    return {"project_key": row[0], "project_name": row[1]}


def find_project_by_id(
    conn,
    project_id: int,
) -> Optional[Dict]:
    cur = conn.cursor()

    cur.execute("""
        SELECT project_key, project_name
        FROM projects
        WHERE id = %s;
    """, (project_id,))

    row = cur.fetchone()
    cur.close()

    if not row:
        return None

    return {
        "project_key": row[0],
        "project_name": row[1],
    }


def find_project_by_name_fuzzy(conn, name_fragment: str) -> List[Dict]:
    cur = conn.cursor()
    cur.execute("""
        SELECT project_key, project_name
        FROM projects
        WHERE project_name ILIKE %s
        ORDER BY project_name;
    """, (f"%{name_fragment}%",))
    rows = cur.fetchall()
    cur.close()

    return [{"project_key": r[0], "project_name": r[1]} for r in rows]

def get_processes(conn, project_key: str) -> List[Dict]:
    cur = conn.cursor()
    cur.execute("""
        SELECT pr.process_name, pr.schedule
        FROM processes pr
        JOIN projects p ON pr.project_id = p.id
        WHERE p.project_key = %s
        ORDER BY pr.process_name;
    """, (project_key,))
    rows = cur.fetchall()
    cur.close()

    return [
        {"process_name": r[0], "schedule": r[1]}
        for r in rows
    ]


def get_schedule(conn, project_key: str, process_name: str) -> Optional[str]:
    cur = conn.cursor()
    cur.execute("""
        SELECT pr.schedule
        FROM processes pr
        JOIN projects p ON pr.project_id = p.id
        WHERE p.project_key = %s
        AND pr.process_name = %s;
    """, (project_key, process_name))

    row = cur.fetchone()
    cur.close()

    return row[0] if row else None

def get_config_value(conn, project_key: str, process_name: str, config_key: str) -> Optional[str]:
    cur = conn.cursor()
    cur.execute("""
        SELECT ce.config_value
        FROM config_entries ce
        JOIN processes pr ON ce.process_id = pr.id
        JOIN projects p ON pr.project_id = p.id
        WHERE p.project_key = %s
        AND pr.process_name = %s
        AND ce.config_key = %s;
    """, (project_key, process_name, config_key))

    row = cur.fetchone()
    cur.close()

    return row[0] if row else None


# ---------------------------------------------------
# Mapping Queries (Single-hop)
# ---------------------------------------------------

def get_field_mappings(
    conn,
    field_name: str,
    oem_name: Optional[str] = None,
    project_key: Optional[str] = None
) -> List[Dict]:

    cur = conn.cursor()

    query = """
        SELECT
            p.project_key,
            pr.process_name,
            am.source_field,
            am.destination_field,
            ms.scenario_name
        FROM attribute_mappings am
        JOIN mapping_scenarios ms ON am.scenario_id = ms.id
        JOIN processes pr ON ms.process_id = pr.id
        JOIN projects p ON pr.project_id = p.id
        JOIN oems o ON p.oem_id = o.id
        WHERE (am.source_field = %s OR am.destination_field = %s)
    """

    params = [field_name, field_name]

    if oem_name:
        query += " AND o.name = %s"
        params.append(oem_name.upper())

    if project_key:
        query += " AND p.project_key = %s"
        params.append(project_key)

    query += " ORDER BY p.project_key, pr.process_name;"

    cur.execute(query, tuple(params))
    rows = cur.fetchall()
    cur.close()

    return [
        {
            "project_key": r[0],
            "process_name": r[1],
            "source_field": r[2],
            "destination_field": r[3],
            "scenario_name": r[4]
        }
        for r in rows
    ]


# ---------------------------------------------------
# Multi-hop Traversal (Recursive CTE)
# ---------------------------------------------------

def get_multi_hop_mappings(
    conn,
    field_name: str,
    oem_name: Optional[str] = None,
    project_key: Optional[str] = None
) -> List[Dict]:

    cur = conn.cursor()

    base_query = """
        WITH RECURSIVE traversal AS (
            SELECT
                am.source_field,
                am.destination_field,
                p.project_key,
                pr.process_name
            FROM attribute_mappings am
            JOIN mapping_scenarios ms ON am.scenario_id = ms.id
            JOIN processes pr ON ms.process_id = pr.id
            JOIN projects p ON pr.project_id = p.id
            JOIN oems o ON p.oem_id = o.id
            WHERE am.source_field = %s
    """

    params = [field_name]

    if oem_name:
        base_query += " AND o.name = %s"
        params.append(oem_name.upper())

    if project_key:
        base_query += " AND p.project_key = %s"
        params.append(project_key)

    base_query += """
            UNION
            SELECT
                am.source_field,
                am.destination_field,
                p.project_key,
                pr.process_name
            FROM attribute_mappings am
            JOIN mapping_scenarios ms ON am.scenario_id = ms.id
            JOIN processes pr ON ms.process_id = pr.id
            JOIN projects p ON pr.project_id = p.id
            JOIN oems o ON p.oem_id = o.id
            JOIN traversal t
                ON am.source_field = t.destination_field
        )
        SELECT * FROM traversal
        ORDER BY project_key, process_name;
    """

    cur.execute(base_query, tuple(params))
    rows = cur.fetchall()
    cur.close()

    return [
        {
            "source_field": r[0],
            "destination_field": r[1],
            "project_key": r[2],
            "process_name": r[3],
        }
        for r in rows
    ]

def get_config_keys(conn, project_key: str, process_name: str):
    cur = conn.cursor()
    cur.execute("""
        SELECT ce.config_key
        FROM config_entries ce
        JOIN processes pr ON ce.process_id = pr.id
        JOIN projects p ON pr.project_id = p.id
        WHERE p.project_key = %s
        AND pr.process_name = %s;
    """, (project_key, process_name))

    rows = cur.fetchall()
    cur.close()

    return [r[0] for r in rows]


def get_all_mapping_fields(conn, oem_name=None, project_key=None):

    query = """
        SELECT DISTINCT am.source_field, am.destination_field
        FROM attribute_mappings am
        JOIN mapping_scenarios ms ON am.scenario_id = ms.id
        JOIN processes pr ON ms.process_id = pr.id
        JOIN projects p ON pr.project_id = p.id
        JOIN oems o ON p.oem_id = o.id
        WHERE 1=1
    """

    params = []

    if oem_name:
        query += " AND o.name = %s"
        params.append(oem_name.upper())

    if project_key:
        query += " AND p.project_key = %s"
        params.append(project_key)

    cur = conn.cursor()
    cur.execute(query, tuple(params))
    rows = cur.fetchall()
    cur.close()

    fields = set()
    for r in rows:
        fields.add(r[0])
        fields.add(r[1])

    return list(fields)

    row = cur.fetchone()
    cur.close()
    return row[0] if row else None

        FROM projects;
    """)
    rows = cur.fetchall()
